## Agents

### archive

`agents.archive`

Archive an agent. Soft-deletes an agent by archiving it. Idempotent: archiving an already archived agent returns 200 with the current agent. Requires the agent:delete API key scope. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.agents.archive({ agentId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `project` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `availableInMCP` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `isArchived` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `versionId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `activeVersionId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `config` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `configHash` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `tools` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `activeVersion` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `parentFolder` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="project full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  type?: string | null
} | null | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="config full type">

```ts

} | null | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="tools full type">

```ts

  code?: string | null,

    name?: string | null
  } | null
}> | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="activeVersion full type">

```ts
    type?: string | null,
```

### create

`agents.create`

Create an agent. Create an agent in your personal project or a team project you have access to. Requires the agent:create API key scope.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.agents.create({ name: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `projectId` | `string` | No | — |
| `config` | `object` | No | Agent configuration. Set name, model, and instructions. An empty model creates an unconfigured draft. |
| `tools` | `object` | No | — |
| `availableInMCP` | `boolean` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="config full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="tools full type">

```ts
Record<string, {
  code: string,
  descriptor: {
    name: string
  }
}>
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `project` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `availableInMCP` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `isArchived` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `versionId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `activeVersionId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `config` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `configHash` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `tools` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `activeVersion` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `parentFolder` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="project full type">

```ts

  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  type?: string | null
} | null | null
```

### delete

`agents.delete`

Delete an agent. Delete an agent and its associated resources. The agent is deleted right away: it does not need to be archived first, and a published agent with live channels is deleted too. This matches the workflow delete endpoint. Requires the agent:delete API key scope. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.agents.delete({ agentId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `availableInMCP` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `versionId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `activeVersionId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### executions.list

`agents.executions.list`

List agent executions. List the runs of one agent that you can see, newest first. Project sessions are visible to every project member, private sessions only to their owner. The response is one cursor page: read the next page with the cursor query parameter. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.agents.executions.list({ agentId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Paginate by setting the cursor parameter to the nextCursor attribute returned by the previous request's response. Default value fetches the first "page" of the collection. See pagination for more detail. |
| `limit` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items to return. |
| `agentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent. |
| `sessionId` | `string` | No | — |
| `status` | `running \| success \| error \| cancelled \| interrupted \| awaiting` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `nextCursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  executionId?: string | null,
  sessionId?: string | null,
  status?: string | null,
  source?: string | null | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  startedAt?: string | null | null,
  stoppedAt?: string | null | null,
  usage?: {
    promptTokens?: number | null | null,
    completionTokens?: number | null | null,
    totalTokens?: number | null | null,
    cost?: number | null | null
  } | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### executions.stop

`agents.executions.stop`

Stop an agent run. Stop a run of this agent. A finished run returns its status. An unknown execution returns 404. Requires the agent:execute API key scope. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.agents.executions.stop({ agentId: 'agt_6TfR2kLm', executionId: 'aexec_9QwE3rTy' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `agentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent. |
| `executionId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent execution. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `executionId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| string \| null` | No | — |

Retrieve an agent. Retrieve one agent by ID. The response carries the agent configuration that the update endpoint writes back. Requires the agent:read API key scope.

### list

`agents.list`

List agents. List the agents in the projects where you hold the agent:list permission. The response is one cursor page: read the next page with the cursor query parameter. Narrow the list to one project with projectId. Requires the agent:list API key scope.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.agents.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Paginate by setting the cursor parameter to the nextCursor attribute returned by the previous request's response. Default value fetches the first "page" of the collection. See pagination for more detail. |
| `limit` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items to return. |
| `projectId` | `string` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `nextCursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  projectId?: string | null,
  availableInMCP?: boolean | null,
  isArchived?: boolean | null,
  versionId?: string | null | null,
  activeVersionId?: string | null | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### publish

`agents.publish`

Publish an agent. Publish an agent. Omit versionId to publish the current draft. Send versionId to publish a saved version. Requires the agent:publish API key scope. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.agents.publish({ agentId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent. |
| `versionId` | `string` | No | The saved version to publish. Omit this field to publish the current draft. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `project` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `availableInMCP` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `isArchived` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `versionId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `activeVersionId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `config` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `configHash` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `tools` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `activeVersion` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `parentFolder` | `object \| null` | No | — |

### run

`agents.run`

Run an agent. Run the latest published version of an agent and wait for the final response. Omit sessionId to start a session. Send the same sessionId to continue that session. The Public API rejects an agent that has no published version, and an agent whose published version uses a tool that needs approval. Runs the agent: its model calls spend the connected model account's tokens. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.agents.run({ agentId: 'example', message: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `agentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent. |
| `message` | `string` | Yes | The message to send to the agent. |
| `sessionId` | `string` | No | The session to continue. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `response` | `string \| null` | No | The final text of the run. |
| `sessionId` | `string \| null` | No | The session this run belongs to. |
| `executionId` | `string \| null` | No | The execution id of this run. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `usage` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `error` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="usage full type">

```ts
{
  promptTokens?: number | null | null,
  completionTokens?: number | null | null,
  totalTokens?: number | null | null,
  cost?: number | null | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### unpublish

Unpublish an agent. Unpublish an agent. This stops the published version and disconnects its live channels. Requires the agent:unpublish API key scope. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

### update

`agents.update`

Update an agent. Replace the configuration of an agent. Send the configuration and the concurrency token you read from the retrieve endpoint: a stale token returns a conflict. Fields the server manages are ignored, not rejected. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.agents.update({
	agentId: 'example',
	config: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent. |
| `config` | `object \| null` | Yes | — |
| `baseConfigHash` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `configHash` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `any` | No | — |
| `name` | `any` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `any` | No | — |
| `project` | `any` | No | — |
| `availableInMCP` | `any` | No | — |
| `isArchived` | `any` | No | — |
| `versionId` | `any` | No | — |
| `activeVersionId` | `any` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `any` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `any` | No | — |
| `tools` | `any` | No | — |
| `activeVersion` | `any` | No | — |
| `parentFolder` | `any` | No | — |
| `parentFolderId` | `any` | No | — |
| `isRunnable` | `any` | No | — |
| `hasPublishHistory` | `any` | No | — |
| `hasVersionHistory` | `any` | No | — |
| `skills` | `any` | No | — |
| `skillHashes` | `any` | No | — |
| `outdatedSkillIds` | `any` | No | — |
| `skillVersions` | `any` | No | — |
| `adopted` | `any` | No | — |

### versions.list

`agents.versions.list`

Retrieve agent version history. Returns a paginated list of saved agent versions for an agent. The response is one cursor page: read the next page with the cursor query parameter. Requires the agent:read API key scope. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.agents.versions.list({ agentId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Paginate by setting the cursor parameter to the nextCursor attribute returned by the previous request's response. Default value fetches the first "page" of the collection. See pagination for more detail. |
| `limit` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items to return. |
| `agentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `nextCursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  versionId?: string | null,
  agentId?: string | null,
  name?: string | null | null,
  description?: string | null | null,
  author?: string | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null,
  isActive?: boolean | null,
  isCurrent?: boolean | null,
  lastPublished?: {
    author?: string | null,
    createdAt?: string | null,
    isLegacy?: boolean | null
  } | null
}[] | null
```

## Ai Preferences

### create

`aiPreferences.create`

Create an AI preference. Create an AI preference for yourself (scope user), for a project (scope project, with projectId), or for the whole instance (scope instance). A preference for the instance or for another user needs the aiPreference:create permission. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.aiPreferences.create({ content: 'example', scope: 'user' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `content` | `string` | Yes | One instruction, written as the user would say it. At most 2000 characters. A scope that already holds 50 preferences refuses a new one, so edit or delete one first. |
| `scope` | `user \| project \| instance` | Yes | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `userId` | `string \| null` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `content` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `scope` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `userId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `source` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

`aiPreferences.delete`

Delete an AI preference. Delete an AI preference. Requires the aiPreference:delete API key scope. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `read`


### list

`aiPreferences.list`

List AI preferences. List the AI preferences you can read: the instance preferences, your own preferences, and the preferences of the projects where you hold the projectAiPreference:list permission. With the aiPreference:list permission, the list also has the preferences of other users. Filter with scope and projectId. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.aiPreferences.list({});
```

| `limit` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items to return. |
| `scope` | `user \| project \| instance` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `nextCursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

```ts
  id?: string | null,
  content?: string | null,
  scope?: string | null,
  userId?: string | null | null,
  projectId?: string | null | null,
  source?: string | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### update

`aiPreferences.update`

Update an AI preference. Replace an AI preference with the full body: content, scope, and the userId or projectId of the scope. A user preference names its user in userId, and a project preference names its project in projectId. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.aiPreferences.update({ aiPreferenceId: 'example', content: 'example', scope: 'user' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `aiPreferenceId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the AI preference. |
| `content` | `string` | Yes | One instruction, written as the user would say it. At most 2000 characters. A scope that already holds 50 preferences refuses a new one, so edit or delete one first. |
| `scope` | `user \| project \| instance` | Yes | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `userId` | `string \| null` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `content` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `scope` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `userId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `source` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

## Community Packages

### install

`communityPackages.install`

Install a community package. Install a community package by npm name and optional version. Requires the communityPackage:install API key scope. Installs an npm package of nodes on the n8n server.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.communityPackages.install({ name: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | npm package name. Unscoped names start with n8n-nodes-. Scoped names use the @[author]/n8n-nodes- form. |
| `version` | `string` | No | Semver version or npm dist-tag, such as latest or beta. When unverified packages are disabled, only versions verified by n8n can be installed. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `packageName` | `string \| null` | No | npm package name |
| `installedVersion` | `string \| null` | No | Currently installed version |
| `authorName` | `string \| null` | No | Package author name |
| `authorEmail` | `string \| null` | No | Package author email |
| `installedNodes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Nodes included in this package |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updateAvailable` | `string \| null` | No | Version available for update, if any |
| `failedLoading` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the package failed to load |
| `policyRestrictions` | `object \| null` | No | Node types from this package that a node type policy blocks. The install does not fail because of a policy. Absent when no policy was evaluated, for example when node type policies are not licensed. An empty nodeTypes list means that no policy blocks a node type from this package. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="installedNodes full type">

```ts
{
  name?: string | null,
  type?: string | null,
  latestVersion?: number | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="policyRestrictions full type">

```ts
{
  nodeTypes?: {
    type?: string | null,
    blockedOn?: string | null,
    optInAvailable?: boolean | null
  }[] | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### list

`communityPackages.list`

List installed community packages. Retrieve all installed community packages with pending update info. Requires the communityPackage:list API key scope.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.communityPackages.list({});
```

**Output:** `object[]`

```ts
  installedVersion?: string | null,
  authorName?: string | null,

    type?: string | null,
    latestVersion?: number | null
  }[] | null,
```

Uninstall a community package. Uninstall a community package by name. Requires the communityPackage:uninstall API key scope. Cannot be undone.

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.communityPackages.uninstall({ name: 'example' });
```

Update a community package. Update an installed community package to a new version. Requires the communityPackage:update API key scope.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.communityPackages.update({ name: 'example' });
```

| `authorName` | `string \| null` | No | Package author name |
| `authorEmail` | `string \| null` | No | Package author email |
| `installedNodes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Nodes included in this package |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updateAvailable` | `string \| null` | No | Version available for update, if any |
| `failedLoading` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the package failed to load |

## Credentials

### create

`credentials.create`

Create a credential. Creates a credential that can be used by nodes of the specified type. Requires the credential:create API key scope. The secret values go in data; n8n encrypts them and never returns them.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.credentials.create({
	name: 'example',
	type: 'example',
	data: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `id` | `string` | No | An unused credential ID of 1–16 letters, digits, underscores, or hyphens. The supplied ID is preserved exactly. Omit to generate an ID. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | Plain text, up to 512 characters. A blank value is saved as null. Ignored when credential descriptions are disabled. |
| `type` | `string` | Yes | — |

### update

`credentials.update`

Update credential by ID. Updates an existing credential. You must be the owner of the credential. Requires the credential:update API key scope.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.credentials.update({ credentialId: 'R2DjclaysHbqn778' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `credentialId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the credential. |
| `name` | `string` | No | The name of the credential |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | Plain text, up to 512 characters. Send null or a blank value to clear it. Omit it to keep the stored value. Ignored when credential descriptions are disabled. |
| `type` | `string` | No | The credential type. If changing type, data must also be provided. |
| `data` | `Record<string, any>` | No | The credential data. Required when changing credential type. Fields also accept n8n expressions, which are strings that start with "=". |
| `isGlobal` | `boolean` | No | Whether this credential is available globally |
| `isResolvable` | `boolean` | No | Whether this credential has resolvable fields |
| `isPartialData` | `boolean` | No | If true, unredacts and merges existing credential data with the provided data. If false, replaces the entire data object. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `isManaged` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `isGlobal` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `isResolvable` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |

| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

## Credential Type Policies

### attachments.get

`credentialTypePolicies.attachments.get`

Preview: Retrieve a scope's attached policy documents. Preview — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Returns every attached policy document and the scope version. Attachments use evaluation order: floor first, then priority ascending within each group. Use this version when replacing the attachments. Needs a licence with feat:typeAvailabilityPolicies (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.credentialTypePolicies.attachments.get({ scopeId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `scopeId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the credential type policy scope. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `attachments` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `version` | `number \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="attachments full type">

```ts
{
  policyId?: string | null,
  rules?: {
    id?: string | null,
    action?: string | null,
    selector?: {
      kind?: string | null,
      value?: string | null
    } | {
      kind?: string | null,
      value?: string | null
    } | {
      kind?: string | null,
      value?: string | null
    } | null
  }[] | null,
  priority?: number | null,
  isFloor?: boolean | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### attachments.replace

`credentialTypePolicies.attachments.replace`

Preview: Replace a scope's attached policy documents. Preview — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Replaces every attachment on a scope. scopeId comes from the scope's GET response once it has been written. Each policyId and each (isFloor, priority) pair must be unique within the list. Needs a licence with feat:typeAvailabilityPolicies (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.credentialTypePolicies.attachments.replace({
	scopeId: 'example',
	attachments: [{ policyId: 'example', priority: 1, isFloor: true }],
	version: 1,
});
```

| `version` | `number` | Yes | — |

### documents.create

`credentialTypePolicies.documents.create`

Preview: Create a credential type policy document. Preview — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Creates a reusable policy document that is not yet attached to any scope. Rule ids must be unique within the list. warnings lists rules that an earlier rule already shadows. Requires the credentialTypePolicy:manage API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:typeAvailabilityPolicies (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.credentialTypePolicies.documents.create({
	rules: [{
		id: 'doc_8f2k1x9q',
		action: 'allow',
		selector: { kind: 'name', value: 'example' },
	}],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `rules` | `object[]` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="rules full type">

```ts
{
  id: string,
  action: allow | deny | delegate,
  selector: {
    kind: name,
    value: string

    kind: package,
    value: string
  } | {
    kind: extends,
    value: string
  }
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `policy` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `warnings` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="policy full type">

```ts
  id?: string | null,
  kind?: string | null,
  rules?: {

      value?: string | null
    } | {

      value?: string | null
    } | null
```

### setInstance

`credentialTypePolicies.setInstance`

Preview: Replace the instance credential type policy. Preview — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Sets the instance default action and replaces the rules of its single policy document, creating both on first write. version must equal the version last read; a stale value is rejected with 409. Needs a licence with feat:typeAvailabilityPolicies (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.credentialTypePolicies.setInstance({
	rules: [{
		id: 'doc_8f2k1x9q',
		action: 'allow',
		selector: { kind: 'name', value: 'example' },
	}],
	defaultAction: 'allow',
	version: 1,
});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `rules` | `object[]` | Yes | — |
| `defaultAction` | `allow \| deny \| delegate` | Yes | — |
| `version` | `number` | Yes | — |

```ts
    value: string

    kind: extends,
    value: string

}[]
```

### setProject

`credentialTypePolicies.setProject`

Preview: Replace a project's credential type policy. Preview — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. ## Data Tables

### columns.create

`dataTables.columns.create`

Add a column to a data table. Add a new column to an existing data table. Requires the dataTableColumn:create API key scope.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.dataTables.columns.create({ dataTableId: 'Ab3dEf6hIj9kLm2N', name: 'example', type: 'string' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `dataTableId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the data table |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | Column name. Must start with a letter; only letters, digits, and underscores after that; maximum 63 characters. |
| `type` | `string \| number \| boolean \| date` | Yes | Column data type |
| `index` | `number` | No | Column position (optional, appended to end if omitted) |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | Column ID |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | Column name |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | Column data type |
| `index` | `number \| null` | No | Column position |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp when the column was created |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp when the column was last updated |
| `dataTableId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the data table this column belongs to |



---

### columns.delete

`dataTables.columns.delete`

Delete a column. Remove a column from a data table. This will also delete all data in the column. Requires the dataTableColumn:delete API key scope. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.dataTables.columns.delete({ dataTableId: 'Ab3dEf6hIj9kLm2N', columnId: 'Zx8cVb7nMq1wEr4T' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `dataTableId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the data table |
| `columnId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the column |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | n8n accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

`dataTables.columns.update`

Update a column. Rename and/or reorder a column in a data table. Requires the dataTableColumn:update API key scope.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.dataTables.columns.update({ dataTableId: 'Ab3dEf6hIj9kLm2N', columnId: 'Zx8cVb7nMq1wEr4T' });
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|

| `name` | `string` | No | Column name. Must start with a letter; only letters, digits, and underscores after that; maximum 63 characters. |

| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp when the column was created |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp when the column was last updated |
| `dataTableId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the data table this column belongs to |

### create

`dataTables.create`

Create a new data table. Create a new data table in your personal project or a team project you have access to. Requires the dataTable:create API key scope.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.dataTables.create({
	name: 'example',
	columns: [{ name: 'example', type: 'string' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | Name of the data table |
| `columns` | `object[]` | Yes | Column definitions for the table |
| `projectId` | `string` | No | ID of the project to create the table in. When omitted, the table is created in the user's personal project. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | ID of a CSV file that you uploaded in the n8n editor. If you set it, n8n fills the new table with the rows from that file. Only a session-authenticated caller can upload a file, so an API-key caller cannot use this field. |
| `hasHeaders` | `boolean` | No | Set to true if the first row of the CSV file holds the column names. Applies only when you set `fileId`. The default is true. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="columns full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  type: string | number | boolean | date,
  csvColumnName?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | Unique identifier for the data table |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | Name of the data table |
| `columns` | `object[] \| null` | No | Column definitions |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the project this table belongs to |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp when the table was created |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp when the table was last updated |
| `sizeBytes` | `number \| null` | No | Physical storage in bytes, including indexes and internal overhead. Not reduced immediately by row deletion, and may be a few seconds stale. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="columns full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  type?: string | null,
  index?: number | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### list

`dataTables.list`

List all data tables. Retrieve a list of all data tables with optional filtering, sorting, and pagination. Each table includes sizeBytes, the value the size sort option orders by. Requires the dataTable:list API key scope.


| Name | Type | Required | Description |

| `cursor` | `string` | No | Paginate by setting the cursor parameter to the nextCursor attribute returned by the previous request's response. Default value fetches the first "page" of the collection. See pagination for more detail. |
| `filter` | `string` | No | JSON string of filter conditions |
| `sortBy` | `name:asc \| name:desc \| createdAt:asc \| createdAt:desc \| updatedAt:asc \| updatedAt:desc \| size:asc \| size:desc` | No | Sort format: field:asc or field:desc |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `nextCursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Paginate through data tables by setting the cursor parameter to a nextCursor attribute returned by a previous request. Default value fetches the first "page" of the collection. |

```ts
    id?: string | null,

    type?: string | null,
    index?: number | null,
    createdAt?: string | null,
    updatedAt?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  projectId?: string | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null,
  sizeBytes?: number | null
}[] | null
```

### rows.clear

`dataTables.rows.clear`

Clear all rows from a data table. Permanently delete all rows from a data table. The table structure is retained. This action cannot be undone. Requires the dataTableRow:delete API key scope. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.dataTables.rows.clear({ dataTableId: 'Ab3dEf6hIj9kLm2N' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `dataTableId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the data table |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `deletedCount` | `number \| null` | No | The number of rows that were deleted |

### rows.delete

`dataTables.rows.delete`

Delete rows from a data table. Delete rows matching filter conditions from a data table. Filter is required to prevent accidental deletion of all data. Requires the dataTableRow:delete API key scope. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.dataTables.rows.delete({ dataTableId: 'Ab3dEf6hIj9kLm2N', filter: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `dataTableId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the data table |
| `filter` | `string` | Yes | JSON string of filter conditions. Required to prevent accidental deletion of all data. |
| `returnData` | `boolean` | No | If true, return the deleted rows. If false, return true on success. |
| `dryRun` | `boolean` | No | If true, preview which rows would be deleted without actually deleting them. |



**Output:** `boolean \| object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
boolean | {
  id?: number | null | null,
  createdAt?: string | null | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null | null,
  dryRunState?: string | null
}[] | {
  id?: number | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

| `dataTableId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the data table |
| `data` | `Record<string, string \| number \| boolean \| any \| string \| any>[]` | Yes | Array of rows to insert. Each row is an object with column names as keys. |

### rows.upsert

`dataTables.rows.upsert`

Upsert a row in a data table. Update an existing row, or insert a new one if no row matches the filter conditions. Requires the dataTableRow:upsert API key scope.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.dataTables.rows.upsert({
	dataTableId: 'Ab3dEf6hIj9kLm2N',
	filter: {
		filters: [{ columnName: 'example', condition: 'eq', value: 'example' }],
	},
	data: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `dataTableId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the data table |
| `filter` | `object` | Yes | Filter conditions to match an existing row. If no row matches, n8n inserts a new row. |
| `data` | `Record<string, string \| number \| boolean \| any \| string \| any>` | Yes | Column values for the row |
| `returnData` | `boolean` | No | Set to true to return the upserted row(s): the matching rows if updated, or the new row if one was inserted. Set to false to return true on success. |
| `dryRun` | `boolean` | No | Set to true to preview the change without saving it. |

```ts
    value: string | number | boolean | string | any | any
  }[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `boolean \| object[]`

```ts
  createdAt?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null
}[]
```

## Discover

### get

`discover.get`

Discover available API capabilities. Returns a filtered capability map based on the caller's API key scopes. Each resource includes the operations and endpoints accessible to the authenticated API key. Use query parameters to narrow the response.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.discover.get({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `include` | `schemas` | No | Include additional data. Use "schemas" to inline request body schemas per endpoint, eliminating the need to fetch the full OpenAPI spec. |
| `resource` | `string` | No | Filter to a specific resource (e.g. "workflow", "tags", "credential"). |
| `operation` | `string` | No | Filter to endpoints with a specific operation (e.g. "read", "create", "list"). |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  scopes?: string[] | null,
  resources?: Record<string, {
    operations?: string[] | null,
    endpoints?: {
      method?: string | null,
      path?: string | null,
      operationId?: string | null,
      requestSchema?: {
      } | null
    }[] | null
  }> | null,
  filters?: Record<string, {
    description?: string | null,
    values?: string[] | null
  }> | null,
  specUrl?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### cancelRun

`evaluations.cancelRun`

Cancel a test run. Cancel a running evaluation test run of a workflow. The user must also have the workflow:execute project scope. Requires the testRun:cancel API key scope. Needs a licence with evaluations (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**


| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `workflowId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the workflow |
| `runId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the test run. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |

## Executions

### delete

`executions.delete`

Delete an execution. Deletes an execution from your instance. Requires the execution:delete API key scope. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.executions.delete({ executionId: 1000 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `executionId` | `number` | Yes | The ID of the execution. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | ID of the deleted execution. This endpoint returns it as a number, where every other execution endpoint returns a string. |
| `finished` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the execution ran to completion. Use `status` instead. |
| `mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `retryOf` | `string \| null \| null` | No | ID of the execution this one is a retry of. |
| `retrySuccessId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | ID of the retry that succeeded, if this execution failed and a retry did not. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `startedAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Null until the execution leaves the queue and starts running. |
| `stoppedAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | When the execution stopped. Null for the `new`, `running` and `unknown` statuses; a `waiting` execution has stopped its current run, so it has a value. |
| `deletedAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Always null. Soft-deleted executions are not returned. |
| `workflowId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `waitTill` | `string \| null \| null` | No | The time at which a waiting execution resumes. |
| `storedAt` | `string \| null` | No | Where the execution data is stored. |
| `tracingContext` | `object \| null` | No | W3C trace context propagated with the execution. |
| `deduplicationKey` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Key used to stop a duplicate execution from being created. |
| `jsonSizeBytes` | `number \| null` | No | Size of the stored execution data in bytes. 0 means unknown. |
| `binaryDataSizeBytes` | `number \| null` | No | Size of the stored binary data in bytes. 0 means unknown. |
| `workflowVersionId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | ID of the workflow version this execution ran. |
| `usedPrivateCredentials` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the execution used credentials that are not shared with the project. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="tracingContext full type">

```ts
{
  traceparent?: string | null,
  tracestate?: string | null
} | null | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### get

`executions.get`

Retrieve an execution. Retrieve an execution from your instance. Requires the execution:read API key scope.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.executions.get({ executionId: 1000 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `executionId` | `number` | Yes | The ID of the execution. |
| `includeData` | `boolean` | No | Whether or not to include the execution's detailed data. |
| `ignoreDataSizeLimit` | `boolean` | No | Whether to return the full execution data even if it exceeds the configured size limit (EXECUTIONS_DATA_MAX_DISPLAY_SIZE). Oversized executions are otherwise returned without their data. |
| `redactExecutionData` | `boolean` | No | Controls execution data redaction. When `true`, execution output data is always redacted. When `false`, requests unredacted (revealed) data — requires the `execution:reveal` scope. When omitted, follows the workflow redaction policy. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `finished` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the execution ran to completion. Use `status` instead. |
| `mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `retryOf` | `string \| null \| null` | No | ID of the execution this one is a retry of. |
| `retrySuccessId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | ID of the retry that succeeded, if this execution failed and a retry did not. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `startedAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Null until the execution leaves the queue and starts running. |
| `stoppedAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | When the execution stopped. Null for the `new`, `running` and `unknown` statuses; a `waiting` execution has stopped its current run, so it has a value. |
| `deletedAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Always null. Soft-deleted executions are not returned. |
| `workflowId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `waitTill` | `string \| null \| null` | No | The time at which a waiting execution resumes. |
| `storedAt` | `string \| null` | No | Where the execution data is stored. |
| `tracingContext` | `object \| null` | No | W3C trace context propagated with the execution. |
| `deduplicationKey` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Key used to stop a duplicate execution from being created. |
| `jsonSizeBytes` | `number \| null` | No | Size of the stored execution data in bytes. 0 means unknown. |
| `binaryDataSizeBytes` | `number \| null` | No | Size of the stored binary data in bytes. 0 means unknown. |
| `workflowVersionId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | ID of the workflow version this execution ran. |
| `usedPrivateCredentials` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the execution used credentials that are not shared with the project. |
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Detailed execution data. Only included when `includeData` is `true`. |
| `customData` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Execution metadata set by the workflow. Only included when `includeData` is `true`. |
| `workflowData` | `object \| null` | No | The workflow as it was saved when the execution ran. Only included when `includeData` is `true`. Older executions carry more fields than newer ones. |
| `dataTooLargeToDisplay` | `boolean \| null` | No | Present and true when the execution data was larger than `EXECUTIONS_DATA_MAX_DISPLAY_SIZE`. `data` is then empty and `jsonSizeBytes` holds the real size. Use `ignoreDataSizeLimit` to read it anyway. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="tracingContext full type">

```ts
{
  traceparent?: string | null,
  tracestate?: string | null
} | null | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  redactionInfo?: {
    isRedacted?: boolean | null,
    reason?: string | null,
    canReveal?: boolean | null
  } | null | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="workflowData full type">

```ts
{
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

```ts
    } | null | null
  } | null,

  } | null,

}[] | null
```

### retry

`executions.retry`

Retry an execution. Retry an execution from your instance. Requires the execution:retry API key scope. Runs the failed execution again, with the same side effects as the first run.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.executions.retry({ executionId: 1000 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `executionId` | `number` | Yes | The ID of the execution. |
| `loadWorkflow` | `boolean` | No | Whether to load the currently saved workflow to execute instead of the one saved at the time of the execution. If set to true, it will retry with the latest version of the workflow. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the new execution that the retry started. |
| `mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `startedAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | The time at which the new execution started. |
| `workflowId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `finished` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the execution ran to completion. Use `status` instead. |
| `retryOf` | `string \| null \| null` | No | ID of the execution this one is a retry of. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `waitTill` | `string \| null \| null` | No | The time at which a waiting execution resumes. |
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | Run data of the new execution. Absent when the API key's user cannot read the executions of the workflow. |
| `workflowData` | `object \| null` | No | The workflow as it was saved when the original execution ran. `loadWorkflow` changes which workflow the retry runs, not what this field reports. Absent when the API key's user cannot read the executions of the workflow. |
| `customData` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Execution metadata carried over from the original execution. Absent when the API key's user cannot read the executions of the workflow. |
| `annotation` | `object \| null` | No | Annotation on the original execution. Absent when it carries none, or when the API key's user cannot read the executions of the workflow. |
| `storedAt` | `string \| null` | No | Where the execution data is stored. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  redactionInfo?: {
    isRedacted?: boolean | null,
    reason?: string | null,
    canReveal?: boolean | null
  } | null | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="workflowData full type">

```ts
{
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="annotation full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null,
  vote?: string | null | null,
  tags?: {
    id?: string | null,
    name?: string | null
  }[] | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `executionId` | `number` | Yes | The ID of the execution. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `startedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The time at which the execution started. |
| `stoppedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The time at which the execution stopped. Absent when no stop time was recorded. |
| `finished` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the execution ran to completion. Use `status` instead. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### stopMany

`executions.stopMany`

Stop multiple executions. Stop multiple executions from your instance based on filter criteria. Requires the execution:stop API key scope.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.executions.stopMany({
	status: ['queued'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `status` | `(queued \| running \| waiting)[]` | Yes | Array of execution statuses to stop. Must include at least one status. |
| `workflowId` | `string` | No | Optional workflow ID to filter executions. If not provided, will stop executions across all accessible workflows. The literal value `all` has the same effect as omitting it. |
| `startedAfter` | `string` | No | Only stop executions that started after this time. |
| `startedBefore` | `string` | No | Only stop executions that started before this time. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `stopped` | `number \| null` | No | The number of executions that were successfully stopped. |

## Folders

### create

`folders.create`

Create a folder. Create a folder within a project. Requires the folder:create API key scope. Needs folders in the licence (the free registered Community licence has them): without it n8n answers 403.

| `projectId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the project, or `personal` for the calling user's own personal project. |

---

### delete

`folders.delete`

Delete a folder. Delete a folder within a project. When transferToFolderId is provided, workflows and sub-folders are moved to the target folder before deletion. When omitted, workflows are moved to the project root and archived, and child folders are deleted. Requires the folder:delete API key scope. Needs folders in the licence (the free registered Community licence has them): without it n8n answers 403. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.folders.delete({ projectId: 'VmwOO9HeTEj20kxM', folderId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `projectId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the project, or `personal` for the calling user's own personal project. |
| `folderId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the folder. |
| `transferToFolderId` | `string` | No | Optional target folder ID to move workflows and sub-folders into before deleting. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | n8n accepted the call; it answered with no body. |



---

### list

`folders.list`

Retrieve folders. Retrieve folders within a project. Supports filtering, sorting, field selection, and pagination. Requires the folder:list API key scope. Needs folders in the licence (the free registered Community licence has them): without it n8n answers 403.

| `skip` | `string` | No | Number of items to skip for pagination. Defaults to 0. |

### attachments.get

Attachments use evaluation order: floor first, then priority ascending within each group. Use this version when replacing the attachments. Needs a licence with feat:typeAvailabilityPolicies (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

Preview: Replace the instance node type policy. Preview — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Sets the instance default action and replaces the rules of its single policy document, creating both on first write. version must equal the version last read; a stale value is rejected with 409. Needs a licence with feat:typeAvailabilityPolicies (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.nodeTypePolicies.setInstance({
	rules: [{
		id: 'doc_8f2k1x9q',
		action: 'allow',
		selector: { kind: 'name', value: 'example' },
	}],
	defaultAction: 'allow',
	version: 1,
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `rules` | `object[]` | Yes | — |
| `defaultAction` | `allow \| deny \| delegate` | Yes | — |
| `version` | `number` | Yes | — |

```ts
  action: allow | deny | delegate,

    kind: package,

}[]
```

### setProject

`nodeTypePolicies.setProject`

Preview: Replace a project's node type policy. Preview — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Sets the project's default action and replaces the rules of its single policy document, creating both on first write. Only a team project can have a policy; a personal project is rejected with 400. await corsair.n8n.api.nodeTypePolicies.setProject({
