## Packages

### export

`packages.export`

Beta: Export agents, workflows, folders, or projects as an n8n package. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Export agents, workflows, and folders, or whole projects, as a gzipped tar archive (.n8np). Provide agentIds/workflowIds/folderIds, or projectIds. Do not combine these two groups. Each exported folder includes its nested folders. Returns a .n8np package (gzip) of the chosen workflows, credentials and data tables.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.packages.export({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `agentIds` | `string[]` | No | IDs of the agents to include. Requires the agents module. |
| `workflowIds` | `string[]` | No | IDs of the workflows to include in the exported package. |
| `folderIds` | `string[]` | No | IDs of the folders to include in the exported package. Each folder is exported with its nested folders. |
| `projectIds` | `string[]` | No | IDs of the projects to include in the exported package. Includes their agents when the agents module is enabled. |
| `includeVariableValues` | `boolean` | No | Whether values of variables referenced by the exported entities are bundled into the package. When `false`, variables still travel as name/type files and are listed in the package requirements, but no values travel with the package. |
| `includeTags` | `boolean` | No | Whether tags assigned to the exported workflows are bundled into the package. When `false`, no tag files, tag references, or tag requirements travel with the package. |
| `dependencyPolicy` | `fail \| reference-only \| include-in-package` | No | Policy for workflow and agent dependencies outside the selected package contents. Includes static workflow references and disabled agent references. `fail` aborts when a required workflow or agent is absent from the package. `include-in-package` adds accessible dependencies recursively. `reference-only` records external requirements without including those definitions. The same policy applies to workflows and agents. |
| `versionPolicy` | `published-strict \| prefer-published \| ignore-unpublished \| latest` | No | Which version of each workflow and agent travels in the package. `latest` exports the current draft. `published-strict` requires a published version. `prefer-published` uses the published version when available and the draft otherwise. `ignore-unpublished` skips unpublished selections. Dependencies that cannot be included cause the export to fail only when `dependencyPolicy` is `fail` or `include-in-package`. With `reference-only`, the export records them as external requirements. The selected version supplies the definition and its references. Workflow names, settings, and tags always use their current values. Agent IDs, names, and MCP availability always use their current values. Skills have no published versions. The package always contains the latest saved version of each skill, also under `published-strict`. The package does not pin skill versions. |
| `credentialExportPolicy` | `expression-values-only \| no-values` | No | Whether expression values from credential data are bundled into the package. `expression-values-only` includes credential fields whose value is an n8n expression (for example `={{ $secrets.apiKey }}`). A literal value travels only when it decides whether another credential field shows (a toggle or an auth type, for example) and an exported expression sits in that field. No other literal value travels. `no-values` keeps credential data out of the package entirely, so each credential file carries only its id, name and type. |
| `includeArchived` | `boolean` | No | Whether folder exports include archived workflows and project exports include archived workflows and agents. When `false` (default) they are left out. When `true` they travel with `isArchived: true` and are archived on import. Workflows listed in `workflowIds` and agents listed in `agentIds` always export, also when archived. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |



---

### import

`packages.import`

Beta: Import an n8n package into a project. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Imports Agent-only, workflow-only, mixed, and whole-project packages. The Agents module must be active for packages that contain Agents. Send the gzip-compressed tar archive (.n8np) as the multipart field package.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.packages.import({
	package: { base64: 'SGVsbG8gZnJvbSBDb3JzYWly', filename: 'notes.txt', contentType: 'text/plain' },
	conflictPolicy: 'new-version',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `package` | `string \| object \| custom` | Yes | Gzip-compressed tar package (`.n8np`). |
| `projectId` | `string` | No | Target project id. Omit or send empty to import into the caller's personal project. |
| `folderId` | `string` | No | Optional folder within the target project. Omit or send empty for project root. |
| `credentialMatchingMode` | `id-only \| name-and-type \| type-only` | No | How credential references in `requirements.credentials` are matched on the target instance. `id-only` (default) matches by id. `name-and-type` matches credentials with the exact same name and type. `type-only` matches any credential of the same type. For `name-and-type` and `type-only`, candidates are ranked by scope — a credential owned by the target project wins over one merely shared into it, which in turn wins over a global credential; if several candidates remain in the winning scope, the most recently updated one is chosen. |
| `credentialMissingMode` | `must-preexist \| create-stub \| create-with-values` | No | What to do when a credential reference cannot be resolved. `create-stub` (default) creates empty credential placeholders in the target project for missing references. `create-with-values` also creates them, seeded from the expression data bundled in the package (see the export option `credentialExportPolicy`); a reference the package carries no data for falls back to an empty stub. A credential created with package data is listed under `credentials.seeded` when the package confirms that all source data was retained. Otherwise, also when the package has no completeness flag, it is listed under `credentials.incomplete` and blocks workflow publishing with `incomplete-credential`. `create-stub` and `create-with-values` require the `credential:create` scope when the import creates a credential. `must-preexist` requires every referenced credential to already exist. |
| `credentialConflictPolicy` | `keep-existing \| overwrite \| fail` | No | What to do when a credential reference matches an existing credential and the package has different values for it. `keep-existing` (default) never changes a matched credential. `overwrite` writes the package name and the expression values from the package to a matched credential that a project of the import owns. It keeps all other stored values: secrets, other fields, the OAuth connection, and array fields. It never changes a global credential that a different project owns, or a managed, end-user (resolvable) or instance-level credential. The import lists the source ids of changed credentials under `credentials.updated`. An API key needs the `credential:update` scope when the import changes a credential. `fail` rejects the import with a 409 (`credential-conflict`) when `overwrite` would change a matched credential. Every policy leaves a matched credential alone when the package values are the same as the stored values. `overwrite` and `fail` compare stored values, so they need the `credential:update` permission on each matched credential that a project of the import owns, also when it would not change. Without it, the import is rejected with `credential-update-forbidden`. |
| `bindings` | `string` | No | Optional JSON object of explicit source→target id bindings, keyed by entity type. Only `credentials` is supported today: send `{"credentials":{"<packageCredentialId>":"<targetCredentialId>"}}` to map credential ids from the package to credential ids on the target instance. These explicit bindings are validated on type and applied before `credentialMatchingMode` resolution runs. |
| `conflictPolicy` | `new-version \| fail \| skip` | Yes | Applies to Agents, workflows and the skills of imported Agents. `new-version` replaces matched definitions and saves a changed skill as a new version. `fail` rejects the import when an Agent or workflow matches, or when a matched skill has other content. A matched skill with the same content is kept. `skip` leaves matched definitions and skills unchanged. All policies create entities and skills that have no match. A new version of a project or instance skill applies to all Agents on the instance that follow the skill, also Agents that are not in the package. A skill conflict under `fail` is an `agent-invalid` issue, so it returns 422. Matched workflows also follow the package archive state under `new-version`; this change needs `workflow:delete`. This required field replaces `workflowConflictPolicy`. The old field is rejected. |
| `publishingPolicy` | `preserve-published-state \| match-source \| publish-all \| unpublish-all` | No | Controls publication of imported Agents and workflows after definitions are saved. `preserve-published-state` (default) keeps new entities unpublished. It republishes an updated entity only if it was published and the package contains the version published at the source. `match-source` publishes the imported definition when the source publishes it. It unpublishes the destination when the source has no published version. Both policies leave publication unchanged when the source publishes a version absent from the package. `publish-all` requests publication of imported entities. `unpublish-all` leaves new entities unpublished and unpublishes updated entities. Skipped entities remain unchanged. Archived workflows remain unpublished. Publication failures do not undo saved definitions. Only the Agent revision saved by the import can be published or unpublished. This field replaces `workflowPublishingPolicy` and `agentPublishingPolicy`. The old fields are rejected. |
| `idPolicy` | `source` | No | Controls IDs for newly created Agents and workflows. Only `source` is supported. It uses the package ID. An ID already used by an unmatched Agent or workflow, in any project, blocks the import. Matched entities keep their destination ID. Agent and workflow identity maps remain separate. This field replaces `workflowIdPolicy` and `agentIdPolicy`. The old fields are rejected. |
| `missingNodeTypeMode` | `fail \| import-anyway` | No | Controls missing node types and versions in workflow nodes and Agent node tools. `fail` (default) rejects the import before writes. The issues identify the affected consumers. `import-anyway` saves the definitions but blocks their publication. This applies to both Agents and workflows. |
| `projectConflictPolicy` | `merge \| fail \| overwrite` | No | What to do when a project the package defines already exists here (matched by id), and — unless `folderConflictPolicy` overrides it — how its contents are treated. `merge` (default) leaves the existing project's name, description, icon and custom span attributes untouched and adds the package's contents alongside. `overwrite` replaces those details with the package's; a detail the package omits is left as it is, not cleared. `fail` rejects the import before anything is written, with one `project-conflict` issue per existing project. A package project id belonging to a personal project, or to a team project the caller cannot update, always rejects the import. A new project needs the `project:create` scope. `overwrite` needs `project:update` only when it changes a detail. Ignored for workflow packages. |
| `folderConflictPolicy` | `merge \| fail \| overwrite` | No | What to do when a package folder already exists at the same position in the target project. Defaults to whatever `projectConflictPolicy` is, so the intent is stated once; a workflow package defines no projects, so it defaults to `merge` there. `merge` reuses the existing folder and merges the package's children into it. `fail` rejects the import. `overwrite` reuses folders as `merge` does and additionally removes workflows the package does not contain — at a project's root and in folders the package defines, leaving target-only folders and their contents alone. Folders it does not define are then removed too, but only once nothing is left inside them. Removals are listed under `removedWorkflows` and `removedFolders`; see `overwriteDeletionPolicy`. A folder whose id sits under a different parent, or belongs to another project, always blocks the import. `overwrite` is project-packages-only. When it removes a workflow, it needs the `workflow:delete` scope. When it removes a folder, it needs `folder:delete`. It is rejected against a `projectConflictPolicy` other than `overwrite`, and blocks up front if the caller cannot delete something it would remove. The import needs the `folder:create` scope only when it creates a folder, and a folders-enabled license only when it creates, changes or removes a folder. |
| `overwriteDeletionPolicy` | `archive \| hard-delete` | No | How `folderConflictPolicy=overwrite` removes a workflow the package does not contain. `archive` (default) archives it, so it stays recoverable along with its execution history. `hard-delete` archives it — the step that unpublishes it, which a delete will not do on its own — and then deletes the workflow and its executions permanently. Each entry in `removedWorkflows` reports what actually happened in its `deletion` field: a `hard-delete` whose row cannot be dropped yet, because unpublishing defers trigger teardown, is left `archived` rather than failing an import whose content is already written. Ignored unless `folderConflictPolicy` is `overwrite`. |
| `dataTableMatchingMode` | `by-id` | No | How data tables referenced by the package's workflows are matched against the target project. `by-id` matches the target-project table with the same id (imported tables keep their source id) and never falls back to name matching, so a match survives a rename on the target. Currently the only mode. |
| `dataTableMissingMode` | `create \| must-preexist \| do-nothing` | No | Controls what happens when a referenced data table has no match in the target project. `create` (default) creates it from the package schema, keeping the source id, with no rows. It requires the `dataTable:create` scope only when it creates a table. A table that already exists needs no scope. `must-preexist` rejects the import. `do-nothing` skips creation of missing tables. Under the `keep-existing`, `fail`, and `overwrite-non-destructive` schema conflict policies, matched tables are still validated for schema compatibility and can still block the import. The import never imports table rows. Matched tables change only under `dataTableSchemaConflictPolicy=overwrite` or `overwrite-non-destructive`, which keep their rows. |
| `dataTableSchemaConflictPolicy` | `keep-existing \| fail \| overwrite \| overwrite-non-destructive` | No | How strictly a matched data table's schema is compared. `keep-existing` (default) accepts a target table that has every package column with the same name and type. The target table can have extra columns. `fail` rejects any difference, including target-only columns. Under `keep-existing` and `fail`, a missing package column or a type mismatch rejects the import, and the target table does not change. `overwrite` changes the matched target table to match the package. It can remove or retype columns. The values in those columns are removed. Rows and other columns are kept. When the package has column ids, a column that is only renamed keeps its values. The changes apply to all workflows that use the table. A blocked `keep-existing`, `fail`, or `overwrite-non-destructive` import lists them in `overwriteChanges`. Requires the `dataTable:update` scope only when a matched table changes. `overwrite-non-destructive` makes the same changes as `overwrite`, but rejects the import and writes nothing when a change removes or retypes a column. A target-only column counts as removed. |
| `variableMissingMode` | `do-nothing \| must-preexist \| create-stub \| create-with-value` | No | Controls what happens when a variable referenced by the package's workflows is absent from the target project and the global scope (lookup order: project, then global). `create-with-value` (default) creates the variable with its package value and lists its name under `variables.created`. When the package carries no value for it — values were excluded at export, or the exported value was itself empty — it creates an empty stub listed under `variables.stubbed`. `do-nothing` imports without creating the variable and lists its name under `variables.missing`. `must-preexist` rejects the import unless every referenced variable already resolves. `create-stub` creates each missing variable with an empty value at the placement scope (see `variableParentPolicy`) and lists the created names under `variables.stubbed`. An import that actually creates a variable requires a license that permits variables and, for API key callers, the `variable:create` scope; a package whose variables all already resolve creates nothing and needs neither. |
| `variableConflictPolicy` | `keep-existing \| overwrite \| fail` | No | What to do when a referenced variable resolves in the target project or global scope but the package bundles a *different* value for it. `keep-existing` (default) leaves the target value untouched and reports the name under `variables.matched`. `overwrite` silently replaces the value of the existing variable at whichever scope it was found — the target project or the global scope, so a global variable other projects also read can be rewritten by this import — and reports the name under `variables.updated`; it needs a license that permits variables, the `variable:update` / `projectVariable:update` permission on that scope, and, for API key callers, the `variable:update` scope. `fail` rejects the import with a 409 instead. Every policy leaves a resolved variable alone when there is nothing to change — either the package bundles no value for it (values were excluded at export, or the exported value was itself empty), or the value it bundles already matches the target's. The reverse does not hold: a resolved variable holding an empty value — a stub an earlier import created, say — still counts as a value, so `overwrite` fills it and `fail` rejects it. Under `overwrite`, a project package whose projects hold *different* values for a name they all resolve to one row — a global none of them shadows, typically — is rejected with a 409: one row cannot carry both values, and the import will not pick for you. Give the projects their own variables in the target, or import them separately. |
| `variableParentPolicy` | `project \| global` | No | Where `create-with-value` and `create-stub` create missing variables for workflow/folder packages. `project` — also the behaviour when the field is omitted — creates them in the import target project (`projectId`, else the caller's personal project). `global` creates them at the global scope. The license and `variable:create` requirements described under `variableMissingMode` apply to both placements. Must be omitted for project packages, which reject it with a 400: their placement follows the package layout, where a variable bundled under a project is created in that project and one bundled at the top level is created globally. |
| `tagMissingMode` | `create \| do-nothing` | No | What to do when a tag referenced by the package's workflows has no tag with the same id on the target instance (tags are matched by source id, never by name). `create` (default) creates the tag globally with its package (source) id and name; when the import would create a tag this needs an API key carrying the `tag:create` scope. `do-nothing` imports the workflows without the missing tags — nothing is created and the dropped names are listed under `tags.skipped`. |
| `tagConflictPolicy` | `skip \| fail \| rename` | No | What to do when a referenced tag conflicts on the target instance — either the same-id target tag carries a different name (rename drift), or the tag's name is already held by a different tag (name collision). `skip` (default) drops the conflicted tags from the import (not created, not renamed, not attached anywhere; the import proceeds and lists them under `tags.skipped`). `fail` rejects the import with a 409. `rename` renames a drifted target tag to the package name, and reconciles a name collision by re-keying the existing tag to the package (source) id — its name, workflow and folder taggings follow; both need an API key carrying the `tag:update` scope when the import would rename or reconcile a tag. A drifted tag whose package name is held by another tag still rejects the import (rename degrades to fail). |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="package full type">

```ts
string | {
  url: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | {
  base64: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | custom
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `package` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `agents` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `counts` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `workflows` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `removedWorkflows` | `object[] \| null` | No | Workflows removed from the target during this import. |
| `removedFolders` | `object[] \| null` | No | Folders the package does not define that reconciliation left empty, removed by `folderConflictPolicy=overwrite`. A folder still holding anything survives, so target-only content is never swept up. Empty under every other policy. |
| `folders` | `object[] \| null` | No | Folder shells created or skipped in the target project. |
| `projects` | `object[] \| null` | No | Project shells created, or matched and then updated or left as-is (see `projectConflictPolicy`). Present for project packages; empty when importing loose workflows/folders. |
| `bindings` | `object \| null` | No | Source id → target id mappings produced during import, one map per entity type. Each value maps an id as it appeared in the package to the id on the target instance. |
| `credentials` | `object \| null` | No | Source credential ids grouped by whether they matched an existing credential, were created as empty stubs, seeded credentials, or incomplete credentials, or matched a credential that this import updated. Full source→target id mapping is in `bindings.credentials`. |
| `dataTables` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `variables` | `object \| null` | No | Resolution of the package's variable requirements. Names only — values never travel in the response. For project packages these arrays are package-level unions of per-destination outcomes and may overlap (for example, a name may be created with a value in one project and stubbed in another); classification under concurrent external writes is best-effort. |
| `tags` | `object \| null` | No | Resolution of the tags referenced by the imported workflows, matched by source id. Tag names only. |

### importSelection

`packages.importSelection`

Beta: Import a selected subset of workflows from an n8n package. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Imports a chosen subset of workflows from one source project of a gzip-compressed tar package (.n8np), instead of the whole package. Send the archive as the multipart field package.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.packages.importSelection({
	package: { base64: 'SGVsbG8gZnJvbSBDb3JzYWly', filename: 'notes.txt', contentType: 'text/plain' },
	selectedProjectId: 'example',
	selectedWorkflowIds: 'example',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `package` | `string \| object \| custom` | Yes | Gzip-compressed tar package (`.n8np`). |
| `selectedProjectId` | `string` | Yes | Id of the single source project the selection is scoped to, as it appears in the package. |
| `selectedWorkflowIds` | `string` | Yes | JSON-encoded array of source workflow ids to import from `selectedProjectId`, e.g. `["wf-1","wf-2"]`. An empty array (`[]`) is allowed — useful for a delete-only request. Ids outside the scoped project are dropped. |
| `deletedWorkflowIds` | `string` | No | Optional JSON-encoded array of destination workflow ids to remove from the scoped project, e.g. `["wf-3"]`. Removal happens even under the additive profile, and the manner of removal follows `overwriteDeletionPolicy` (archive by default). An absent id is a no-op unless it is also a selected destination. Must not contain a selected workflow's destination id. Needs the `workflow:delete` scope. |
| `conflictPolicy` | `new-version \| fail \| skip` | No | What happens when a selected workflow matches an existing workflow by source id in the target project. `new-version` (default) updates it and creates a new version; `fail` rejects the import when any matching workflow exists; `skip` leaves matching workflows unchanged. An archive state change also needs the `workflow:delete` scope. |
| `idPolicy` | `source` | No | Controls IDs for newly created workflows. Only `source` is supported. It uses the package ID. An ID already used by an unmatched workflow, in any project, blocks the import. Matched workflows keep their destination ID. |
| `overwriteDeletionPolicy` | `archive \| hard-delete` | No | How the ids in `deletedWorkflowIds` are removed. `archive` (default) archives each one, so it stays recoverable along with its execution history. `hard-delete` archives it — the step that unpublishes it, which a delete will not do on its own — and then deletes the workflow and its executions permanently. Each entry in `removedWorkflows` reports what actually happened in its `deletion` field: a `hard-delete` whose row cannot be dropped yet, because unpublishing defers trigger teardown, is left `archived` rather than failing an import whose content is already written. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="package full type">

```ts
string | {
  url: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | {
  base64: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | custom
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `package` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `agents` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `counts` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `workflows` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `removedWorkflows` | `object[] \| null` | No | Workflows removed from the target during this import. |
| `removedFolders` | `object[] \| null` | No | Folders the package does not define that reconciliation left empty, removed by `folderConflictPolicy=overwrite`. A folder still holding anything survives, so target-only content is never swept up. Empty under every other policy. |
| `folders` | `object[] \| null` | No | Folder shells created or skipped in the target project. |
| `projects` | `object[] \| null` | No | Project shells created, or matched and then updated or left as-is (see `projectConflictPolicy`). Present for project packages; empty when importing loose workflows/folders. |
| `bindings` | `object \| null` | No | Source id → target id mappings produced during import, one map per entity type. Each value maps an id as it appeared in the package to the id on the target instance. |
| `credentials` | `object \| null` | No | Source credential ids grouped by whether they matched an existing credential, were created as empty stubs, seeded credentials, or incomplete credentials, or matched a credential that this import updated. Full source→target id mapping is in `bindings.credentials`. |
| `dataTables` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `variables` | `object \| null` | No | Resolution of the package's variable requirements. Names only — values never travel in the response. For project packages these arrays are package-level unions of per-destination outcomes and may overlap (for example, a name may be created with a value in one project and stubbed in another); classification under concurrent external writes is best-effort. |
| `tags` | `object \| null` | No | Resolution of the tags referenced by the imported workflows, matched by source id. Tag names only. |

```ts
{
  sourceN8nVersion?: string | null,
  sourceId?: string | null
} | null
```

## Projects

### create

`projects.create`

Create a project. Create a team project on your instance. Requires the project:create API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:projectRole:admin (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.projects.create({ name: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `id` | `string` | No | Project ID. The server generates an ID when omitted. An ID that is in use returns HTTP 409. |
| `icon` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `customTelemetryTags` | `object[]` | No | — |

```ts
  value: string,
  color?: string
```

```ts
  key: string,
  value: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

## Promotions

### environments.addProject

`promotions.environments.addProject`

Beta: Link a project to a promotion environment. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Links a team project to a project-scoped environment. A project can have one environment for each direction. A direction without a project environment uses the instance environment. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.environments.addProject({ promotionEnvironmentId: 'example', projectId: 'VmwOO9HeTEj20kxM' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `promotionEnvironmentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion environment. |
| `projectId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the project. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `environmentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

`promotions.environments.apply`

| `promotionEnvironmentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion environment. |
| `expectedSource` | `object` | No | — |
| `expectedPreviewRevision` | `string` | No | The `revision` of the reviewed preview. The operation builds the preview again. When it differs, the operation returns status `preview-changed` and changes nothing. |

### environments.connect

`promotions.environments.connect`

Beta: Connect an environment. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Prepares the environment on the instance that handles the request. For a Git provider, this clones the configured branch into local storage. Safe to call repeatedly. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.environments.connect({ promotionEnvironmentId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `promotionEnvironmentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion environment. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `direction` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `branchName` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `hasCheckout` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |



---

### environments.continueApply

`promotions.environments.continueApply`

Beta: Continue Apply after binding setup. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Rechecks the configured source and current target bindings. Send expectedSource with the environmentId, branchName, and full commitSha from the reviewed Apply result. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.environments.continueApply({
	promotionEnvironmentId: 'example',
	expectedSource: { environmentId: 'example', branchName: 'example', commitSha: '9fceb02d0ae598e95dc970b74767f19372d61af8' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `promotionEnvironmentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion environment. |
| `expectedSource` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `expectedPreviewRevision` | `string` | No | The `revision` of the reviewed preview. The operation builds the preview again. When it differs, the operation returns status `preview-changed` and changes nothing. |
| `confirmDestructiveChanges` | `boolean` | No | Also applies destructive data table changes, which a `destructive-change` conflict reports: removed or retyped columns. The values in those columns are removed. Rows and other columns are kept. On a full apply, also deletes the team projects that a `project-deletion` conflict reports, with everything in them. Without `expectedPreviewRevision`, the confirmation covers every such change and deletion when Continue runs, including those that are not in the reviewed result. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="expectedSource full type">

```ts
{
  environmentId: string,
  branchName: string,
  commitSha: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `object`

### environments.create

`promotions.environments.create`

Beta: Create a promotion environment. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Creates an environment for one direction on an existing provider. The settings must match the direction. A GitLab remote URL must belong to the saved provider. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.environments.create({
	name: 'example',
	scope: 'instance',
	providerId: 'example',
	remote: { schemaVersion: 1, remoteUrl: 'https://example.com/callback' },
	direction: 'apply',
	settings: { schemaVersion: 1, branchName: 'example' },
});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `scope` | `instance \| projects` | Yes | — |
| `providerId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `remote` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `direction` | `apply \| promote` | Yes | — |
| `settings` | `object` | Yes | — |

```ts
}
```

```ts
} | {

}
```

### environments.delete

`promotions.environments.delete`

Beta: Delete a promotion environment. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Deletes the environment with its project links and local checkout. Its provider is kept. Nothing in the Git repository changes. The open promotion reviews of the environment close in n8n. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.environments.delete({ promotionEnvironmentId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `promotionEnvironmentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion environment. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | n8n accepted the call; it answered with no body. |



---

### environments.disconnect

`promotions.environments.disconnect`

Beta: Remove an environment's local checkout. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Removes the local checkout on this instance, asks the other main instances to remove theirs, and keeps the environment, its credentials, and the trusted SSH host keys. Another operation on the same environment returns 423. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.environments.disconnect({ promotionEnvironmentId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `promotionEnvironmentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion environment. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `direction` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `branchName` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `hasCheckout` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |

### environments.getChangeDetails

`promotions.environments.getChangeDetails`

Beta: Read the content of resources in the whole-instance Apply preview. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Returns the content before and after the change for up to 50 resources of the whole-instance Apply preview. Send the commitSha and the resource key values from the preview. The branch side is read at that commit. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.environments.getChangeDetails({
	promotionEnvironmentId: 'example',
	commitSha: '9fceb02d0ae598e95dc970b74767f19372d61af8',
	keys: ['example'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `promotionEnvironmentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion environment. |
| `commitSha` | `string \| null` | Yes | The `commitSha` of the preview. `null` when the preview had none. |
| `keys` | `string[]` | Yes | The `key` of each resource to read, from the preview. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `details` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `missingKeys` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |

### environments.listMissingProjects

`promotions.environments.listMissingProjects`

Beta: List package projects that are missing on this instance. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Reads the package at the Apply branch tip and returns its team projects whose IDs do not exist on this instance. Projects are matched by ID, not by name. An ID that a personal project uses counts as existing. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.environments.listMissingProjects({ promotionEnvironmentId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `promotionEnvironmentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion environment. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `git` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="git full type">

```ts
{
  commitSha?: string | null,
  branchName?: string | null
} | null | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  icon?: {
    type?: string | null,
    value?: string | null
  } | null,
  description?: string | null,
  customTelemetryTags?: {
    key?: string | null,
    value?: string | null
  }[] | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.environments.listProjects({ promotionEnvironmentId: 'example' });
```

### environments.promote

`promotions.environments.promote`

Beta: Promote all team projects. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Exports every team project, commits it, and pushes to the configured base branch or a new timestamped branch. When the requireApproval setting is alwaysRequired, it always pushes a new branch, whatever the environment setting is. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.environments.promote({ promotionEnvironmentId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `promotionEnvironmentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion environment. |
| `commitMessage` | `string` | No | Message for the promotion commit. If you omit it, the commit uses a generated message. |
| `force` | `boolean` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `counts` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `git` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `review` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `warnings` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="counts full type">

```ts
{
  workflows?: number | null,
  folders?: number | null,
  credentials?: number | null,
  dataTables?: number | null,
  variables?: number | null,
  tags?: number | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="git full type">

```ts
{
  commitSha?: string | null,
  branchName?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="review full type">

```ts
{
  reviewId?: string | null,
  number?: number | null,
  webUrl?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="warnings full type">

```ts
{
  message?: string | null,
  linkUrl?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### environments.removeProject

`promotions.environments.removeProject`

Beta: Unlink a project from a promotion environment. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Requires the promotions:manageEnvironments API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.environments.removeProject({ promotionEnvironmentId: 'example', projectId: 'VmwOO9HeTEj20kxM' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `promotionEnvironmentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion environment. |
| `projectId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the project. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | n8n accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

### environments.update

`promotions.environments.update`

Beta: Update a promotion environment. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Updates the name, provider, remote, or settings. A remote replaces the whole saved remote, and settings replace the whole saved settings. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.environments.update({ promotionEnvironmentId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `promotionEnvironmentId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion environment. |
| `name` | `string` | No | — |
| `providerId` | `string` | No | — |
| `remote` | `object` | No | — |
| `settings` | `object` | No | — |

### projects.apply

`promotions.projects.apply`

Beta: Apply a selection of a project's workflows and folders. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Applies selected changes from the project's Apply branch. The project's name, icon, description and telemetry tags always come from the branch, so a request without workflowIds and folderIds applies only the project. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.projects.apply({ projectId: 'VmwOO9HeTEj20kxM' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `projectId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the project. |
| `workflowIds` | `string[]` | No | — |
| `folderIds` | `string[]` | No | Folders of the project on the branch. Apply creates a selected folder, or moves and renames it to match the branch. |
| `expectedSource` | `object` | No | — |
| `expectedPreviewRevision` | `string` | No | The `revision` of the reviewed preview. The operation builds the preview again. When it differs, the operation returns status `preview-changed` and changes nothing. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="expectedSource full type">

```ts
{
  environmentId: string,
  branchName: string,
  commitSha: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `object`

### projects.continueApply

`promotions.projects.continueApply`

Beta: Continue applying a selection after binding setup. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Rechecks the project's Apply source and the current bindings for the selection. Resend the full selection: workflowIds and folderIds. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.projects.continueApply({
	projectId: 'VmwOO9HeTEj20kxM',
	expectedSource: { environmentId: 'example', branchName: 'example', commitSha: '9fceb02d0ae598e95dc970b74767f19372d61af8' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `projectId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the project. |
| `workflowIds` | `string[]` | No | — |
| `folderIds` | `string[]` | No | Folders of the project on the branch. Apply creates a selected folder, or moves and renames it to match the branch. |
| `expectedSource` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `expectedPreviewRevision` | `string` | No | The `revision` of the reviewed preview. The operation builds the preview again. When it differs, the operation returns status `preview-changed` and changes nothing. |
| `confirmDestructiveChanges` | `boolean` | No | Also applies destructive data table changes, which a `destructive-change` conflict reports: removed or retyped columns. The values in those columns are removed. Rows and other columns are kept. On a full apply, also deletes the team projects that a `project-deletion` conflict reports, with everything in them. Without `expectedPreviewRevision`, the confirmation covers every such change and deletion when Continue runs, including those that are not in the reviewed result. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="expectedSource full type">

```ts
{
  environmentId: string,
  branchName: string,
  commitSha: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `object`

### projects.getChangeDetails

`promotions.projects.getChangeDetails`

Beta: Read the content of changed resources. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Returns the content before and after the change for up to 50 resources of a change preview. Send the commitSha and the resource key values from the preview. The branch side is read at that commit. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.projects.getChangeDetails({
	projectId: 'VmwOO9HeTEj20kxM',
	direction: 'apply',
	commitSha: '9fceb02d0ae598e95dc970b74767f19372d61af8',
	keys: ['example'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `projectId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the project. |
| `direction` | `apply \| promote` | Yes | The direction of the promotion: apply or promote. |
| `commitSha` | `string \| null` | Yes | The `commitSha` of the preview. `null` when the preview had none. |
| `keys` | `string[]` | Yes | The `key` of each resource to read, from the preview. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `details` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `missingKeys` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |

### projects.listChanges

`promotions.projects.listChanges`

Beta: Preview the changes of a project in one direction. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Compares a team project on this instance with the branch of its promotion environment for that direction. For promote the preview is what a promotion sends to the branch, and the key needs the promotions:promote scope. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.projects.listChanges({ projectId: 'VmwOO9HeTEj20kxM', direction: 'apply' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `projectId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the project. |
| `direction` | `apply \| promote` | Yes | The direction of the promotion: apply or promote. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `commitSha` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `source` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `environment` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `resources` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `dependencies` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `blocker` | `object \| null` | No | Set when an open review already holds the changes of this project. The preview then lists no resources. Only a Promote that opens a review on a new branch sets it. |
| `check` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `revision` | `string \| null` | No | A hash of what this preview shows. Send it as `expectedPreviewRevision` with Promote or Apply to run only when the preview did not change. |

### projects.promote

`promotions.projects.promote`

Beta: Promote a selection of a project's workflows and folders. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Promotes a chosen set of a team project's workflows and folders to the branch of the project's Promote environment. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.projects.promote({ projectId: 'VmwOO9HeTEj20kxM' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `projectId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the project. |
| `workflowIds` | `string[]` | No | — |
| `folderIds` | `string[]` | No | Folders of the project to promote. Promote writes a selected folder with its current name and place, even when it is empty. |
| `credentialIds` | `string[]` | No | Credentials that the project owns, to promote without a selected workflow that uses them. Promote writes a credential under the folder of its owner project. It never writes secret values. |
| `commitMessage` | `string` | No | Message for the promotion commit. If you omit it, the commit uses a generated message. |
| `expectedPreviewRevision` | `string` | No | The `revision` of the reviewed preview. The operation builds the preview again. When it differs, the operation returns status `preview-changed` and changes nothing. |



**Output:** `object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  environmentId?: string | null,
  counts?: {
    workflows?: number | null,
    folders?: number | null,
    credentials?: number | null,
    dataTables?: number | null,
    variables?: number | null,
    tags?: number | null
  } | null,
  git?: {
    commitSha?: string | null,
    branchName?: string | null
  } | null,
  review?: {
    reviewId?: string | null,
    number?: number | null,
    webUrl?: string | null
  } | null,
  warnings?: {
    message?: string | null,
    linkUrl?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  status?: string | null
} | {
  status?: string | null,
  environmentId?: string | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### providers.create

`promotions.providers.create`

Beta: Create a promotion provider. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Creates a provider and its authentication material. A Git host provider validates authenticated API access before it saves. For GitLab, the service uses n8n as the Git transport username. Its auth.username is optional. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.providers.create({
	name: 'example',
	type: 'git',
	auth: { authType: 'ssh-key' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `type` | `git \| gitlab` | Yes | — |
| `auth` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `config` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="auth full type">

```ts
{
  authType: ssh-key,
  keyType?: ed25519 | rsa
} | {
  authType: token,
  username?: string,
  password: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="config full type">

```ts
{
  schemaVersion: 1,
  baseUrl: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `provider` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `publicKey` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="provider full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  type?: string | null,
  authType?: string | null,
  config?: {
    schemaVersion?: number | null,
    publicKey?: string | null,
    keyType?: string | null
  } | {
    schemaVersion?: number | null
  } | {
    schemaVersion?: number | null,
    baseUrl?: string | null
  } | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### providers.delete

`promotions.providers.delete`

Beta: Delete a promotion provider. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. A provider used by an environment cannot be deleted. Requires the promotions:manageProviders API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.providers.delete({ promotionProviderId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `promotionProviderId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion provider. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | n8n accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

### providers.listBranches

`promotions.providers.listBranches`

Beta: List branches of a promotion repository. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Lists branches of the selected GitLab repository and identifies its default branch. Pass the URL-encoded fullPath from repository discovery, or the repositoryPath of a saved GitLab environment, as repositoryId. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.providers.listBranches({ promotionProviderId: 'example', repositoryId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `limit` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items to return. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Paginate by setting the cursor parameter to the nextCursor attribute returned by the previous request's response. Default value fetches the first "page" of the collection. See pagination for more detail. |
| `promotionProviderId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion provider. |
| `repositoryId` | `string` | Yes | The URL-encoded fullPath of the repository. A GitLab project ID is also accepted. |
| `search` | `string` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `nextCursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  name?: string | null,
  isDefault?: boolean | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### providers.listRepositories

`promotions.providers.listRepositories`

Beta: List repositories of a promotion provider. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Lists repositories where the GitLab token identity has at least the Developer role. The token also needs Git write scope for promotions. Protected branches can restrict pushes. Use remoteUrl as an environment remote. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

| `promotionProviderId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion provider. |
| `search` | `string` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|

| `nextCursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

### providers.update

`promotions.providers.update`

Beta: Update a promotion provider. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Renames the provider, and replaces its credentials when auth is sent. A Git host provider can also update its base URL. A different API destination requires auth in the same request. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

### reviews.get

`promotions.reviews.get`

Beta: Retrieve a promotion review. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Returns one promotion review with the same fields as the list. Reads the merge request from the Git host now. Returns 404 when the Inbox of the key owner does not show the review. Requires the promotions:readReviews API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.reviews.get({ promotionReviewId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `promotionReviewId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the promotion review. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | The Inbox tab of the review. `closed` covers the merged and the closed reviews. |
| `reviewState` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the review. `open` and `changes_requested` come from the Git host. `merged` and `closed` are final. |
| `reviewType` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `title` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `webUrl` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `branchName` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `environmentName` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `requester` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `approver` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `remoteError` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Why the Git host data is missing. Then `title`, `webUrl` and `headCommitSha` are NULL, and `hasConflicts` is false without a check. NULL when the Git host answered. |
| `headCommitSha` | `string \| null \| null` | No | The head of the merge request on the Git host. It differs from `runCommitSha` after a push on the Git host. |
| `runCommitSha` | `string \| null \| null` | No | The commit that n8n pushed in the newest promotion. |
| `hasConflicts` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="requester full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  firstName?: string | null | null,
  lastName?: string | null | null,
  email?: string | null | null
} | null | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="approver full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  firstName?: string | null | null,
  lastName?: string | null | null,
  email?: string | null | null
} | null | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

|------|------|----------|-------------|

```ts
  } | null | null,
```

### settings.get

`promotions.settings.get`

Beta: Get the promotion settings. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Returns who can promote, approve, and apply in their own projects, and whether every promote needs a review. Instance owners and admins can always promote, approve, and apply. Requires the securitySettings:manage API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.settings.get({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `whoCanPromote` | `string \| null` | No | Who can promote a project and see its promotion reviews. `instanceAdmins`: only instance owners and admins. `projectAdmins`: also project admins, in their projects. `projectAdminsAndEditors`: also project admins and editors, in their projects. |
| `whoCanApprove` | `string \| null` | No | Who can see and approve the promotion reviews of a project. `instanceAdmins`: only instance owners and admins. `projectAdmins`: also project admins, in their projects. `projectAdminsAndEditors`: also project admins and editors, in their projects. |
| `whoCanApply` | `string \| null` | No | Who can apply changes into a project. `instanceAdmins`: only instance owners and admins. `projectAdmins`: also project admins, in their projects. `projectAdminsAndEditors`: also project admins and editors, in their projects. |
| `requireApproval` | `string \| null` | No | Whether a promote needs a review. `whenConfigured`: each Promote environment uses its own review setting. `alwaysRequired`: every promote pushes a new branch. With a `gitlab` provider, it also opens a merge request. With a `git` provider, n8n cannot see the review and cannot stop direct pushes. |



---

### settings.update

`promotions.settings.update`

Beta: Update the promotion settings. Beta — breaking changes may still occur without major version bump. Changes who can promote, approve, and apply in their own projects, and whether every promote needs a review. Send only the settings to change. A setting that you leave out keeps its value. Needs a licence with feat:promotions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.promotions.settings.update({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `whoCanPromote` | `instanceAdmins \| projectAdmins \| projectAdminsAndEditors` | No | Who can promote a project and see its promotion reviews. `instanceAdmins`: only instance owners and admins. `projectAdmins`: also project admins, in their projects. `projectAdminsAndEditors`: also project admins and editors, in their projects. |
| `whoCanApprove` | `instanceAdmins \| projectAdmins \| projectAdminsAndEditors` | No | Who can see and approve the promotion reviews of a project. `instanceAdmins`: only instance owners and admins. `projectAdmins`: also project admins, in their projects. `projectAdminsAndEditors`: also project admins and editors, in their projects. |
| `whoCanApply` | `instanceAdmins \| projectAdmins \| projectAdminsAndEditors` | No | Who can apply changes into a project. `instanceAdmins`: only instance owners and admins. `projectAdmins`: also project admins, in their projects. `projectAdminsAndEditors`: also project admins and editors, in their projects. |
| `requireApproval` | `whenConfigured \| alwaysRequired` | No | Whether a promote needs a review. `whenConfigured`: each Promote environment uses its own review setting. `alwaysRequired`: every promote pushes a new branch. With a `gitlab` provider, it also opens a merge request. With a `git` provider, n8n cannot see the review and cannot stop direct pushes. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `whoCanPromote` | `string \| null` | No | Who can promote a project and see its promotion reviews. `instanceAdmins`: only instance owners and admins. `projectAdmins`: also project admins, in their projects. `projectAdminsAndEditors`: also project admins and editors, in their projects. |
| `whoCanApprove` | `string \| null` | No | Who can see and approve the promotion reviews of a project. `instanceAdmins`: only instance owners and admins. `projectAdmins`: also project admins, in their projects. `projectAdminsAndEditors`: also project admins and editors, in their projects. |
| `whoCanApply` | `string \| null` | No | Who can apply changes into a project. `instanceAdmins`: only instance owners and admins. `projectAdmins`: also project admins, in their projects. `projectAdminsAndEditors`: also project admins and editors, in their projects. |
| `requireApproval` | `string \| null` | No | Whether a promote needs a review. `whenConfigured`: each Promote environment uses its own review setting. `alwaysRequired`: every promote pushes a new branch. With a `gitlab` provider, it also opens a merge request. With a `git` provider, n8n cannot see the review and cannot stop direct pushes. |

## Role Mapping Rules

### create

`roleMappingRules.create`

Create a role-mapping rule. Creates a rule that maps an identity-provider claim expression to a role. Set type to instance for a rule granting a global role, or project for a rule granting a project role on the projects named in projectIds. Omitting order appends the rule to the end of the evaluation order for its type. Needs a licence with provisioning (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.roleMappingRules.create({ expression: 'example', role: 'example', type: 'instance' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `expression` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `role` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `type` | `instance \| project` | Yes | — |
| `order` | `number` | No | — |
| `projectIds` | `string[]` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `expression` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `role` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `order` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `projectIds` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### delete

`roleMappingRules.delete`

Delete a role-mapping rule. Deletes a role-mapping rule. The remaining rules of the same type close the gap, so their order values stay a contiguous sequence starting at 0. Requires the roleMappingRule:delete API key scope. Needs a licence with provisioning (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.roleMappingRules.delete({ roleMappingRuleId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `roleMappingRuleId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the role mapping rule. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `expression` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `role` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `order` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `projectIds` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### list

`roleMappingRules.list`

Retrieve role-mapping rules. Returns the configured role-mapping rules. order is the rule's evaluation position within its own type, so instance and project rules each have their own sequence starting at 0 — filter by type to retrieve a single evaluation order. Requires the roleMappingRule:list API key scope. Needs a licence with provisioning (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.roleMappingRules.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `limit` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items to return. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Paginate by setting the cursor parameter to the nextCursor attribute returned by the previous request's response. Default value fetches the first "page" of the collection. See pagination for more detail. |
| `type` | `instance \| project` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `nextCursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  expression?: string | null,
  role?: string | null,
  type?: string | null,
  order?: number | null,
  projectIds?: string[] | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### move

`roleMappingRules.move`

Move a role-mapping rule. Changes a rule's position in the evaluation order for its type. targetIndex is the desired 0-based position within the rule's own type sequence; a value beyond the last position moves the rule to the end. Requires the roleMappingRule:update API key scope. Needs a licence with provisioning (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.roleMappingRules.move({ roleMappingRuleId: 'example', targetIndex: 1 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `roleMappingRuleId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the role mapping rule. |
| `targetIndex` | `number` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `expression` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `role` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `order` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `projectIds` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### update

`roleMappingRules.update`

Update a role-mapping rule. Updates a rule's claim expression, role, and/or project assignments. A rule's type cannot be changed once created, so type isn't accepted here, and reordering is handled by the move endpoint. Requires the roleMappingRule:update API key scope. Needs a licence with provisioning (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.roleMappingRules.update({ roleMappingRuleId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `roleMappingRuleId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the role mapping rule. |
| `expression` | `string` | No | — |
| `role` | `string` | No | — |
| `projectIds` | `string[]` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `expression` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `role` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `order` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `projectIds` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
