## Roles

### create

`roles.create`

Create a custom role. Creates a custom role. Set roleType to global for an instance-wide role or project for a project role. Requires one of these API key scopes: role:manage, role:manageProject. Needs a licence with feat:customRoles (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.roles.create({
	displayName: 'example',
	roleType: 'project',
	scopes: ['example'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `displayName` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `description` | `string` | No | — |
| `roleType` | `project \| global` | Yes | — |
| `scopes` | `string[]` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `slug` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `displayName` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `description` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `systemRole` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `roleType` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `scopes` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### delete

`roles.delete`

Delete a custom role. Deletes a custom role. System roles cannot be deleted. A role with users assigned cannot be deleted unless reassignRoleSlug is set to move those users to another role first. Requires one of these API key scopes: role:manage, role:manageProject. Needs a licence with feat:customRoles (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.roles.delete({ roleSlug: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `roleSlug` | `string` | Yes | The slug of the role. |
| `reassignRoleSlug` | `global:admin \| global:member \| global:chatUser \| string` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `slug` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `displayName` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `description` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `systemRole` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `roleType` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `scopes` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

## Settings

### ldap.get

`settings.ldap.get`

Retrieve the LDAP configuration. Retrieve the current LDAP configuration, including every field exposed in the UI. The binding admin password is redacted on read. Requires the LDAP feature to be licensed. Requires the ldap:manage API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:ldap (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.ldap.get({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `loginEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether LDAP login is enabled. |
| `loginLabel` | `string \| null` | No | Label shown on the LDAP login button. |
| `connectionUrl` | `string \| null` | No | LDAP server URL. |
| `allowUnauthorizedCerts` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to allow unauthorized (self-signed) certificates. |
| `connectionSecurity` | `string \| null` | No | TLS/SSL security mode for the LDAP connection. |
| `connectionPort` | `number \| null` | No | LDAP server port. |
| `baseDn` | `string \| null` | No | Base DN for LDAP search queries. |
| `bindingAdminDn` | `string \| null` | No | DN of the LDAP admin user for binding. |
| `bindingAdminPassword` | `string \| null` | No | Password for the LDAP admin user. Redacted on GET; returns the blanking placeholder when a password is stored, empty string when unset. Send the blanking placeholder from a prior GET to keep the stored password unchanged. |
| `firstNameAttribute` | `string \| null` | No | LDAP attribute mapped to the user's first name. |
| `lastNameAttribute` | `string \| null` | No | LDAP attribute mapped to the user's last name. |
| `emailAttribute` | `string \| null` | No | LDAP attribute mapped to the user's email. |
| `loginIdAttribute` | `string \| null` | No | LDAP attribute used for login (usually the same as emailAttribute). |
| `ldapIdAttribute` | `string \| null` | No | LDAP attribute that uniquely identifies a user. |
| `userFilter` | `string \| null` | No | Additional LDAP filter to apply when searching for users. Use an empty string for no additional filter. |
| `synchronizationEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether automatic LDAP synchronization is enabled. |
| `synchronizationInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Interval in minutes between automatic synchronizations. Ignored if synchronizationEnabled is false. |
| `searchPageSize` | `number \| null` | No | Number of LDAP entries to fetch per search page. |
| `searchTimeout` | `number \| null` | No | LDAP search timeout in seconds. |
| `enforceEmailUniqueness` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to enforce that email addresses are unique across LDAP users. When true, if two users have the same email, only the first will be imported. |
| `linkExistingAccountsByEmail` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether an LDAP user may be linked to an existing n8n account that has the same email. When false, login and synchronization skip such users. Optional on PUT; the stored value is kept when omitted. |

### ldap.listSyncs

`settings.ldap.listSyncs`

Retrieve LDAP synchronization history. Retrieve the history of LDAP synchronizations, most recent first. Requires the LDAP feature to be licensed. Requires the ldap:sync API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:ldap (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.ldap.listSyncs({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `limit` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items to return. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Paginate by setting the cursor parameter to the nextCursor attribute returned by the previous request's response. Default value fetches the first "page" of the collection. See pagination for more detail. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `nextCursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Paginate through the synchronization history by setting the cursor parameter to the nextCursor attribute returned by the previous request. A null value means there are no more records. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null,
  runMode?: string | null,
  status?: string | null,
  startedAt?: string | null,
  endedAt?: string | null,
  scanned?: number | null,
  created?: number | null,
  updated?: number | null,
  disabled?: number | null,
  error?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### ldap.runSync

`settings.ldap.runSync`

Trigger an LDAP synchronization. Manually trigger an LDAP synchronization. The response returns the new sync history record. Requires the LDAP feature to be licensed. Requires the ldap:sync API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:ldap (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.ldap.runSync({ type: 'live' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `type` | `live \| dry` | Yes | Type of synchronization. 'live' applies changes to the database. 'dry' performs a test run and does not persist changes. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | Unique identifier for this sync run. |
| `runMode` | `string \| null` | No | Whether the sync was a dry run or applied live. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | Status of the synchronization (for example, success or error). |
| `startedAt` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp when the synchronization started. |
| `endedAt` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp when the synchronization completed. |
| `scanned` | `number \| null` | No | Number of LDAP entries scanned during synchronization. |
| `created` | `number \| null` | No | Number of new users created during synchronization. |
| `updated` | `number \| null` | No | Number of existing users updated during synchronization. |
| `disabled` | `number \| null` | No | Number of users disabled during synchronization. |
| `error` | `string \| null` | No | Error message if the synchronization failed. Empty string if successful. |



---

### ldap.update

`settings.ldap.update`

Set the LDAP configuration. Replace the LDAP configuration with the provided full object (partial updates are not supported). For bindingAdminPassword, submit the blanking placeholder from a prior GET to keep the stored password unchanged. Requires the LDAP feature to be licensed. Needs a licence with feat:ldap (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.ldap.update({
	loginEnabled: true,
	loginLabel: 'example',
	connectionUrl: 'https://example.com/callback',
	allowUnauthorizedCerts: true,
	connectionSecurity: 'none',
	connectionPort: 1,
	baseDn: 'example',
	bindingAdminDn: 'example',
	bindingAdminPassword: 'example',
	firstNameAttribute: 'example',
	lastNameAttribute: 'example',
	emailAttribute: 'ada@example.com',
	loginIdAttribute: 'example',
	ldapIdAttribute: 'example',
	userFilter: 'example',
	synchronizationEnabled: true,
	synchronizationInterval: 1,
	searchPageSize: 1,
	searchTimeout: 1,
	enforceEmailUniqueness: true,
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `loginEnabled` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether LDAP login is enabled. Setting this to false is destructive — it deletes all stored LDAP user identities and disables synchronization. |
| `loginLabel` | `string` | Yes | Label shown on the LDAP login button. |
| `connectionUrl` | `string` | Yes | LDAP server URL. |
| `allowUnauthorizedCerts` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether to allow unauthorized (self-signed) certificates. |
| `connectionSecurity` | `none \| tls \| startTls` | Yes | TLS/SSL security mode for the LDAP connection. |
| `connectionPort` | `number` | Yes | LDAP server port. |
| `baseDn` | `string` | Yes | Base DN for LDAP search queries. |
| `bindingAdminDn` | `string` | Yes | DN of the LDAP admin user for binding. |
| `bindingAdminPassword` | `string` | Yes | Password for the LDAP admin user. To keep an existing password unchanged, submit the blanking placeholder from a prior GET response. Use an empty string to clear the password. |
| `firstNameAttribute` | `string` | Yes | LDAP attribute mapped to the user's first name. |
| `lastNameAttribute` | `string` | Yes | LDAP attribute mapped to the user's last name. |
| `emailAttribute` | `string` | Yes | LDAP attribute mapped to the user's email. |
| `loginIdAttribute` | `string` | Yes | LDAP attribute used for login (usually the same as emailAttribute). |
| `ldapIdAttribute` | `string` | Yes | LDAP attribute that uniquely identifies a user. |
| `userFilter` | `string` | Yes | Additional LDAP filter to apply when searching for users. Use an empty string for no additional filter. |
| `synchronizationEnabled` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether automatic LDAP synchronization is enabled. |
| `synchronizationInterval` | `number` | Yes | Interval in minutes between automatic synchronizations. Ignored if synchronizationEnabled is false. |
| `searchPageSize` | `number` | Yes | Number of LDAP entries to fetch per search page. |
| `searchTimeout` | `number` | Yes | LDAP search timeout in seconds. |
| `enforceEmailUniqueness` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether to enforce that email addresses are unique across LDAP users. When true, if two users have the same email, only the first will be imported. |
| `linkExistingAccountsByEmail` | `boolean` | No | Whether an LDAP user may be linked to an existing n8n account that has the same email. When false, login and synchronization skip such users. Optional on PUT; the stored value is kept when omitted. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `loginEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether LDAP login is enabled. |
| `loginLabel` | `string \| null` | No | Label shown on the LDAP login button. |
| `connectionUrl` | `string \| null` | No | LDAP server URL. |
| `allowUnauthorizedCerts` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to allow unauthorized (self-signed) certificates. |
| `connectionSecurity` | `string \| null` | No | TLS/SSL security mode for the LDAP connection. |
| `connectionPort` | `number \| null` | No | LDAP server port. |
| `baseDn` | `string \| null` | No | Base DN for LDAP search queries. |
| `bindingAdminDn` | `string \| null` | No | DN of the LDAP admin user for binding. |
| `bindingAdminPassword` | `string \| null` | No | Password for the LDAP admin user. Redacted on GET; returns the blanking placeholder when a password is stored, empty string when unset. Send the blanking placeholder from a prior GET to keep the stored password unchanged. |
| `firstNameAttribute` | `string \| null` | No | LDAP attribute mapped to the user's first name. |
| `lastNameAttribute` | `string \| null` | No | LDAP attribute mapped to the user's last name. |
| `emailAttribute` | `string \| null` | No | LDAP attribute mapped to the user's email. |
| `loginIdAttribute` | `string \| null` | No | LDAP attribute used for login (usually the same as emailAttribute). |
| `ldapIdAttribute` | `string \| null` | No | LDAP attribute that uniquely identifies a user. |
| `userFilter` | `string \| null` | No | Additional LDAP filter to apply when searching for users. Use an empty string for no additional filter. |
| `synchronizationEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether automatic LDAP synchronization is enabled. |
| `synchronizationInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Interval in minutes between automatic synchronizations. Ignored if synchronizationEnabled is false. |
| `searchPageSize` | `number \| null` | No | Number of LDAP entries to fetch per search page. |
| `searchTimeout` | `number \| null` | No | LDAP search timeout in seconds. |
| `enforceEmailUniqueness` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to enforce that email addresses are unique across LDAP users. When true, if two users have the same email, only the first will be imported. |
| `linkExistingAccountsByEmail` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether an LDAP user may be linked to an existing n8n account that has the same email. When false, login and synchronization skip such users. Optional on PUT; the stored value is kept when omitted. |

### logStreaming.createDestination

`settings.logStreaming.createDestination`

Create a log streaming destination. Create a log streaming destination. The destination takes effect exactly as it would from the UI, using the same validation. Requires the Log Streaming feature to be licensed. Needs a licence with feat:logStreaming (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.logStreaming.createDestination({ type: 'webhook' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `label` | `string` | No | Human-readable name shown in the UI. |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | Whether the destination currently receives events. |
| `subscribedEvents` | `string[]` | No | Event names (or group prefixes, e.g. `n8n.workflow`, which matches all `n8n.workflow.*` events) this destination receives. Retrieve the full list of streamable event names from `GET /settings/log-streaming/event-types`. |
| `anonymizeAuditMessages` | `boolean` | No | Whether audit message payloads are anonymized before being sent. |
| `circuitBreaker` | `object` | No | Circuit-breaker tuning for delivery. After repeated failures the destination stops sending for a cool-down window instead of hammering an unhealthy target. All fields are optional; sensible defaults apply when omitted. |
| `type` | `webhook \| syslog \| sentry` | Yes | Sends each event as an HTTP request to a URL. |
| `url` | `string` | No | Target URL that receives the event payload. |
| `method` | `string` | No | HTTP method used for the request. |
| `sendHeaders` | `boolean` | No | Whether to attach custom headers to the request. |
| `specifyHeaders` | `string` | No | How custom headers are provided — `keypair` or `json`. |
| `headerParameters` | `object` | No | Key-value pairs used when the matching `specify*` field is `keypair`. |
| `jsonHeaders` | `string` | No | Custom headers as a JSON string when `specifyHeaders` is `json`. |
| `sendQuery` | `boolean` | No | Whether to attach query parameters to the request. |
| `specifyQuery` | `string` | No | How query parameters are provided — `keypair` or `json`. |
| `queryParameters` | `object` | No | Key-value pairs used when the matching `specify*` field is `keypair`. |
| `jsonQuery` | `string` | No | Query parameters as a JSON string when `specifyQuery` is `json`. |
| `options` | `object` | No | Additional HTTP request options. |
| `host` | `string` | No | Syslog server host. |
| `port` | `number` | No | Syslog server port. |
| `protocol` | `udp \| tcp \| tls` | No | Transport protocol. |
| `facility` | `number` | No | Syslog facility code (0–23, per RFC 5424). Common values: 0 Kernel, 1 User, 3 System, 13 Audit, 14 Alert, 16 Local0, 17 Local1, 18 Local2, 19 Local3, 20 Local4, 21 Local5, 22 Local6, 23 Local7. |
| `app_name` | `string` | No | Application name reported in the syslog message. |
| `tlsCa` | `string` | No | PEM-encoded CA certificate used when `protocol` is `tls`. |
| `dsn` | `string` | No | Sentry DSN the events are sent to. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="circuitBreaker full type">

```ts
{
  maxFailures?: number,
  failureWindow?: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="headerParameters full type">

```ts
{
  parameters: {
    name: string,
    value: string | number | boolean | any | any
  }[]
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="queryParameters full type">

```ts
{
  parameters: {
    name: string,
    value: string | number | boolean | any | any
  }[]
}
```
</Accordion>

```ts

  timeout?: number,

  queryParameterArrays?: indices | brackets | repeat,
  redirect?: {
    redirect: {
      followRedirects?: boolean,
      maxRedirects?: number
    }
  },
  proxy?: {
    proxy: {
      protocol: https | http,
      host: string,
      port: number
    }
  },
  socket?: {
    keepAlive?: boolean,
    maxSockets?: number,
    maxFreeSockets?: number
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### logStreaming.deleteDestination

`settings.logStreaming.deleteDestination`

Delete a log streaming destination. Remove a log streaming destination. Requires the Log Streaming feature to be licensed. When destinations are managed via environment variables, the delete is rejected with 409 and nothing is removed; reads still return the current values. Requires the eventBusDestination:delete API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:logStreaming (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.logStreaming.deleteDestination({ destinationId: 'example' });
```

### logStreaming.testDestination

`settings.logStreaming.testDestination`

Send a test message to a log streaming destination. Send a test message to the destination to verify it is reachable and configured correctly. Requires the Log Streaming feature to be licensed. ### logStreaming.updateDestination

`settings.logStreaming.updateDestination`

Update a log streaming destination. Replace an existing log streaming destination. The update takes effect exactly as it would from the UI, using the same validation. Requires the Log Streaming feature to be licensed. Needs a licence with feat:logStreaming (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.logStreaming.updateDestination({ destinationId: 'example', type: 'webhook' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `destinationId` | `string` | Yes | The id of the log streaming destination. |
| `label` | `string` | No | Human-readable name shown in the UI. |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | Whether the destination currently receives events. |
| `subscribedEvents` | `string[]` | No | Event names (or group prefixes, e.g. `n8n.workflow`, which matches all `n8n.workflow.*` events) this destination receives. Retrieve the full list of streamable event names from `GET /settings/log-streaming/event-types`. |
| `anonymizeAuditMessages` | `boolean` | No | Whether audit message payloads are anonymized before being sent. |
| `circuitBreaker` | `object` | No | Circuit-breaker tuning for delivery. After repeated failures the destination stops sending for a cool-down window instead of hammering an unhealthy target. All fields are optional; sensible defaults apply when omitted. |
| `type` | `webhook \| syslog \| sentry` | Yes | Sends each event as an HTTP request to a URL. |
| `url` | `string` | No | Target URL that receives the event payload. |
| `method` | `string` | No | HTTP method used for the request. |
| `sendHeaders` | `boolean` | No | Whether to attach custom headers to the request. |
| `specifyHeaders` | `string` | No | How custom headers are provided — `keypair` or `json`. |
| `headerParameters` | `object` | No | Key-value pairs used when the matching `specify*` field is `keypair`. |
| `jsonHeaders` | `string` | No | Custom headers as a JSON string when `specifyHeaders` is `json`. |
| `sendQuery` | `boolean` | No | Whether to attach query parameters to the request. |
| `specifyQuery` | `string` | No | How query parameters are provided — `keypair` or `json`. |
| `queryParameters` | `object` | No | Key-value pairs used when the matching `specify*` field is `keypair`. |
| `jsonQuery` | `string` | No | Query parameters as a JSON string when `specifyQuery` is `json`. |
| `options` | `object` | No | Additional HTTP request options. |
| `host` | `string` | No | Syslog server host. |
| `port` | `number` | No | Syslog server port. |
| `protocol` | `udp \| tcp \| tls` | No | Transport protocol. |
| `facility` | `number` | No | Syslog facility code (0–23, per RFC 5424). Common values: 0 Kernel, 1 User, 3 System, 13 Audit, 14 Alert, 16 Local0, 17 Local1, 18 Local2, 19 Local3, 20 Local4, 21 Local5, 22 Local6, 23 Local7. |
| `app_name` | `string` | No | Application name reported in the syslog message. |
| `tlsCa` | `string` | No | PEM-encoded CA certificate used when `protocol` is `tls`. |
| `dsn` | `string` | No | Sentry DSN the events are sent to. |

```ts
  failureWindow?: number
}
```

### oidc.get

`settings.oidc.get`

Retrieve the OIDC SSO configuration. Retrieve the current OIDC SSO configuration, including every field exposed in the UI. The client secret is redacted on read and is never echoed back in plaintext. Requires the OIDC feature to be licensed. Requires the oidc:manage API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:oidc (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.oidc.get({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `clientId` | `string \| null` | No | The client ID issued when registering n8n with the OIDC provider. |
| `clientSecret` | `string \| null` | No | The client secret issued when registering n8n with the OIDC provider. Redacted on read when set; never echoed back in plaintext. |
| `discoveryEndpoint` | `string \| null` | No | The OIDC provider's well-known discovery endpoint. |
| `loginEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether OIDC single sign-on is enabled. |
| `prompt` | `string \| null` | No | The prompt parameter to use when authenticating with the OIDC provider. |
| `authenticationContextClassReference` | `string[] \| null` | No | ACR values to include in the authorization request (acr_values parameter), in order of preference. |
| `additionalScopes` | `string \| null` | No | Additional scopes to request, space separated. n8n always requests `openid`, `profile` and `email`. |
| `emailVerifiedRequired` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the identity provider must assert that the user's email address is verified before the login is accepted. When disabled, only an explicit negative assertion is rejected. |
| `rpInitiatedLogoutEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether signing out of n8n also ends the session at the OIDC provider via RP-Initiated Logout. When disabled, sign-out is local to n8n only. |



---

### oidc.update

`settings.oidc.update`

Set the OIDC SSO configuration. Set the OIDC SSO configuration. The update takes effect exactly as it would from the UI, using the same validation. clientId, clientSecret and discoveryEndpoint are required; submit the redacted client secret sentinel to keep the stored secret unchanged. Requires the OIDC feature to be licensed. Needs a licence with feat:oidc (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.oidc.update({
	clientId: 'example',
	clientSecret: 'example',
	discoveryEndpoint: 'example',
	loginEnabled: true,
	prompt: 'none',
	authenticationContextClassReference: ['example'],
	additionalScopes: 'example',
	emailVerifiedRequired: true,
	rpInitiatedLogoutEnabled: true,
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `clientId` | `string` | Yes | The client ID issued when registering n8n with the OIDC provider. |
| `clientSecret` | `string` | Yes | The client secret issued when registering n8n with the OIDC provider. Submit the redacted sentinel value returned on read to keep the stored secret unchanged. |
| `discoveryEndpoint` | `string` | Yes | The OIDC provider's well-known discovery endpoint. |
| `loginEnabled` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether OIDC single sign-on is enabled. |
| `prompt` | `none \| login \| consent \| select_account \| create` | Yes | The prompt parameter to use when authenticating. |
| `authenticationContextClassReference` | `string[]` | Yes | ACR values to include in the authorization request (acr_values parameter), in order of preference. Use an empty array when unused. |
| `additionalScopes` | `string` | Yes | Additional scopes to request, space separated. n8n always requests `openid`, `profile` and `email`. Use an empty string when unused. |
| `emailVerifiedRequired` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether the identity provider must assert that the user's email address is verified before the login is accepted. When disabled, only an explicit negative assertion is rejected. |
| `rpInitiatedLogoutEnabled` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether signing out of n8n also ends the session at the OIDC provider via RP-Initiated Logout. When disabled, sign-out is local to n8n only. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `clientId` | `string \| null` | No | The client ID issued when registering n8n with the OIDC provider. |
| `clientSecret` | `string \| null` | No | The client secret issued when registering n8n with the OIDC provider. Redacted on read when set; never echoed back in plaintext. |
| `discoveryEndpoint` | `string \| null` | No | The OIDC provider's well-known discovery endpoint. |
| `loginEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether OIDC single sign-on is enabled. |
| `prompt` | `string \| null` | No | The prompt parameter to use when authenticating with the OIDC provider. |
| `authenticationContextClassReference` | `string[] \| null` | No | ACR values to include in the authorization request (acr_values parameter), in order of preference. |
| `additionalScopes` | `string \| null` | No | Additional scopes to request, space separated. n8n always requests `openid`, `profile` and `email`. |
| `emailVerifiedRequired` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the identity provider must assert that the user's email address is verified before the login is accepted. When disabled, only an explicit negative assertion is rejected. |
| `rpInitiatedLogoutEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether signing out of n8n also ends the session at the OIDC provider via RP-Initiated Logout. When disabled, sign-out is local to n8n only. |

### otel.get

`settings.otel.get`

Retrieve the OpenTelemetry configuration. Retrieve the current OpenTelemetry configuration, including every field exposed in the UI. Requires the otel:manage API key scope.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.otel.get({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether OpenTelemetry tracing is enabled. |
| `exporterProtocol` | `string \| null` | No | The wire protocol used to export spans. Collectors conventionally serve OTLP/HTTP on port 4318 and OTLP/gRPC on port 4317. The endpoint scheme (`http://` or `https://`) controls TLS for both protocols. Optional: a write that omits it selects `http/protobuf`, but an instance that sets this field to another value with an environment variable rejects that write with 409. |
| `exporterEndpoint` | `string \| null` | No | The base URL of the OTLP collector to export traces to. The value must be an `http://` or `https://` URL, because the scheme selects TLS — for gRPC, `https://` uses SSL and `http://` is plaintext. |
| `exporterTracingPath` | `string \| null` | No | The path appended to the endpoint for the OTLP traces signal. Ignored when `exporterProtocol` is `grpc`, which takes no URL path. |
| `exporterServiceName` | `string \| null` | No | The `service.name` resource attribute reported on every span. |
| `exporterHeaders` | `string \| null` | No | Additional headers sent to the OTLP collector, as a single string of comma-separated `key=value` pairs (e.g. `authorization=Bearer my-token,x-tenant-id=acme`). Whitespace around each key and value is trimmed; a value may contain spaces but not commas. Use an empty string when unused. |
| `tracesSampleRate` | `number \| null` | No | The ratio of traces to sample, between 0 (none) and 1 (all). |
| `startupConnectivityTimeoutMs` | `number \| null` | No | How long, in milliseconds, to wait when checking the collector is reachable. Also used as the timeout for the test-trace endpoint. |
| `includeNodeSpans` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to emit a span for each node execution in addition to the workflow span. |
| `emitWorkflowStartSpan` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to emit a `workflow.execute.started` span with a duration of 0 ms when each execution or resumed segment starts. Optional: a write that omits it selects `false`, but an instance that sets this field to `true` with an environment variable rejects that write with 409. |
| `emitNodeStartSpan` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to emit a `node.execute.started` span with a duration of 0 ms when each node span starts. This doubles the number of node spans. Has no effect when `includeNodeSpans` is false. Optional: a write that omits it selects `false`, but an instance that sets this field to `true` with an environment variable rejects that write with 409. |
| `injectOutbound` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to inject trace context headers into outbound HTTP requests made by nodes. |
| `productionExecutionsOnly` | `boolean \| null` | No | When true, only production executions of published (active) workflows are traced, not manual/test runs. |



---

### otel.testTrace

`settings.otel.testTrace`

Test the connection to an OTLP collector. Send a single test span to the given OTLP collector and report whether it was accepted. This tests the supplied connection details without changing the stored configuration. Fields managed declaratively via environment variables are overridden with their effective value before the test is sent.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.otel.testTrace({
	exporterEndpoint: 'https://otel.example.com:4318',
	exporterTracingPath: 'example',
	exporterServiceName: 'example',
	exporterHeaders: 'example',
	startupConnectivityTimeoutMs: 1,
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `exporterProtocol` | `http/protobuf \| grpc` | No | The wire protocol used to send the test span. Collectors conventionally serve OTLP/HTTP on port 4318 and OTLP/gRPC on port 4317. The endpoint scheme (`http://` or `https://`) controls TLS for both protocols. Optional: omitting it selects `http/protobuf`, unless an environment variable manages this field, which then supplies the value. |
| `exporterEndpoint` | `string` | Yes | The base URL of the OTLP collector to export traces to. The value must be an `http://` or `https://` URL, because the scheme selects TLS — for gRPC, `https://` uses SSL and `http://` is plaintext. |
| `exporterTracingPath` | `string` | Yes | The path appended to the endpoint for the OTLP traces signal. Ignored when `exporterProtocol` is `grpc`, which takes no URL path. |
| `exporterServiceName` | `string` | Yes | The `service.name` resource attribute reported on the test span. |
| `exporterHeaders` | `string` | Yes | Additional headers sent to the OTLP collector, as a single string of comma-separated `key=value` pairs (e.g. `authorization=Bearer my-token,x-tenant-id=acme`). Whitespace around each key and value is trimmed; a value may contain spaces but not commas. Use an empty string when unused. |
| `startupConnectivityTimeoutMs` | `number` | Yes | How long, in milliseconds, to wait for the collector to respond. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the test span was accepted by the collector. |
| `error` | `string \| null` | No | The error reported by the collector or exporter. Present only when `success` is false. |



---

### otel.update

`settings.otel.update`

Set the OpenTelemetry configuration. Set the OpenTelemetry configuration. This is a full replacement: every field must be provided, and a partial body is rejected. The exceptions are exporterProtocol, which defaults to http/protobuf when omitted, and emitWorkflowStartSpan and emitNodeStartSpan, which default to false when omitted.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.otel.update({
	enabled: true,
	exporterEndpoint: 'https://otel.example.com:4318',
	exporterTracingPath: 'example',
	exporterServiceName: 'example',
	exporterHeaders: 'example',
	tracesSampleRate: 1,
	startupConnectivityTimeoutMs: 1,
	includeNodeSpans: true,
	injectOutbound: true,
	productionExecutionsOnly: true,
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `enabled` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether OpenTelemetry tracing is enabled. |
| `exporterProtocol` | `http/protobuf \| grpc` | No | The wire protocol used to export spans. Collectors conventionally serve OTLP/HTTP on port 4318 and OTLP/gRPC on port 4317. The endpoint scheme (`http://` or `https://`) controls TLS for both protocols. Optional: a write that omits it selects `http/protobuf`, but an instance that sets this field to another value with an environment variable rejects that write with 409. |
| `exporterEndpoint` | `string` | Yes | The base URL of the OTLP collector to export traces to. The value must be an `http://` or `https://` URL, because the scheme selects TLS — for gRPC, `https://` uses SSL and `http://` is plaintext. |
| `exporterTracingPath` | `string` | Yes | The path appended to the endpoint for the OTLP traces signal. Ignored when `exporterProtocol` is `grpc`, which takes no URL path. |
| `exporterServiceName` | `string` | Yes | The `service.name` resource attribute reported on every span. |
| `exporterHeaders` | `string` | Yes | Additional headers sent to the OTLP collector, as a single string of comma-separated `key=value` pairs (e.g. `authorization=Bearer my-token,x-tenant-id=acme`). Whitespace around each key and value is trimmed; a value may contain spaces but not commas. Use an empty string when unused. |
| `tracesSampleRate` | `number` | Yes | The ratio of traces to sample, between 0 (none) and 1 (all). |
| `startupConnectivityTimeoutMs` | `number` | Yes | How long, in milliseconds, to wait when checking the collector is reachable. Also used as the timeout for the test-trace endpoint. |
| `includeNodeSpans` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether to emit a span for each node execution in addition to the workflow span. |
| `emitWorkflowStartSpan` | `boolean` | No | Whether to emit a `workflow.execute.started` span with a duration of 0 ms when each execution or resumed segment starts. Optional: a write that omits it selects `false`, but an instance that sets this field to `true` with an environment variable rejects that write with 409. |
| `emitNodeStartSpan` | `boolean` | No | Whether to emit a `node.execute.started` span with a duration of 0 ms when each node span starts. This doubles the number of node spans. Has no effect when `includeNodeSpans` is false. Optional: a write that omits it selects `false`, but an instance that sets this field to `true` with an environment variable rejects that write with 409. |
| `injectOutbound` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether to inject trace context headers into outbound HTTP requests made by nodes. |
| `productionExecutionsOnly` | `boolean` | Yes | When true, only production executions of published (active) workflows are traced, not manual/test runs. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether OpenTelemetry tracing is enabled. |
| `exporterProtocol` | `string \| null` | No | The wire protocol used to export spans. Collectors conventionally serve OTLP/HTTP on port 4318 and OTLP/gRPC on port 4317. The endpoint scheme (`http://` or `https://`) controls TLS for both protocols. Optional: a write that omits it selects `http/protobuf`, but an instance that sets this field to another value with an environment variable rejects that write with 409. |
| `exporterEndpoint` | `string \| null` | No | The base URL of the OTLP collector to export traces to. The value must be an `http://` or `https://` URL, because the scheme selects TLS — for gRPC, `https://` uses SSL and `http://` is plaintext. |
| `exporterTracingPath` | `string \| null` | No | The path appended to the endpoint for the OTLP traces signal. Ignored when `exporterProtocol` is `grpc`, which takes no URL path. |
| `exporterServiceName` | `string \| null` | No | The `service.name` resource attribute reported on every span. |
| `exporterHeaders` | `string \| null` | No | Additional headers sent to the OTLP collector, as a single string of comma-separated `key=value` pairs (e.g. `authorization=Bearer my-token,x-tenant-id=acme`). Whitespace around each key and value is trimmed; a value may contain spaces but not commas. Use an empty string when unused. |
| `tracesSampleRate` | `number \| null` | No | The ratio of traces to sample, between 0 (none) and 1 (all). |

Optional: a write that omits it selects `false`, but an instance that sets this field to `true` with an environment variable rejects that write with 409. |
| `emitNodeStartSpan` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to emit a `node.execute.started` span with a duration of 0 ms when each node span starts. This doubles the number of node spans. ### rateLimits.get

`settings.rateLimits.get`

Retrieve the Public API rate-limit settings. Retrieve the Public API rate-limit settings. envManagedFields lists the fields that are set by environment variables and can't be changed through the API. Requests to this route count against the rate limits. Requires the securitySettings:manage API key scope.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.rateLimits.get({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether Public API rate limiting is enabled. |
| `globalReadLimit` | `number \| null` | No | Read requests allowed for one API key across all endpoints. Set 0 for no limit. |
| `globalReadWindowSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | The window for the global read limit, in seconds. |
| `globalWriteLimit` | `number \| null` | No | Write requests allowed for one API key across all endpoints. Set 0 for no limit. |
| `globalWriteWindowSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | The window for the global write limit, in seconds. |
| `endpointReadLimit` | `number \| null` | No | Read requests allowed for one API key on one endpoint. Set 0 for no limit. |
| `endpointReadWindowSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | The window for the endpoint read limit, in seconds. |
| `endpointWriteLimit` | `number \| null` | No | Write requests allowed for one API key on one endpoint. Set 0 for no limit. |
| `endpointWriteWindowSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | The window for the endpoint write limit, in seconds. |
| `envManagedFields` | `string[] \| null` | No | Fields controlled by environment variables, including _FILE variables. These fields return their effective values. A PUT can repeat these values but cannot change them. |



---

### rateLimits.update

`settings.rateLimits.update`

Set the Public API rate-limit settings. Set the Public API rate-limit settings. You can send a GET response back as the body, and envManagedFields is ignored. When rate limiting is enabled, at least one limit must be greater than 0.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.rateLimits.update({
	enabled: true,
	globalReadLimit: 1,
	globalReadWindowSeconds: 2,
	globalWriteLimit: 1,
	globalWriteWindowSeconds: 2,
	endpointReadLimit: 1,
	endpointReadWindowSeconds: 2,
	endpointWriteLimit: 1,
	endpointWriteWindowSeconds: 2,
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `enabled` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether Public API rate limiting is enabled. |
| `globalReadLimit` | `number` | Yes | Read requests allowed for one API key across all endpoints. Set 0 for no limit. |
| `globalReadWindowSeconds` | `number` | Yes | The window for the global read limit, in seconds. |
| `globalWriteLimit` | `number` | Yes | Write requests allowed for one API key across all endpoints. Set 0 for no limit. |
| `globalWriteWindowSeconds` | `number` | Yes | The window for the global write limit, in seconds. |
| `endpointReadLimit` | `number` | Yes | Read requests allowed for one API key on one endpoint. Set 0 for no limit. |
| `endpointReadWindowSeconds` | `number` | Yes | The window for the endpoint read limit, in seconds. |
| `endpointWriteLimit` | `number` | Yes | Write requests allowed for one API key on one endpoint. Set 0 for no limit. |
| `endpointWriteWindowSeconds` | `number` | Yes | The window for the endpoint write limit, in seconds. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether Public API rate limiting is enabled. |
| `globalReadLimit` | `number \| null` | No | Read requests allowed for one API key across all endpoints. Set 0 for no limit. |
| `globalReadWindowSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | The window for the global read limit, in seconds. |
| `globalWriteLimit` | `number \| null` | No | Write requests allowed for one API key across all endpoints. Set 0 for no limit. |
| `globalWriteWindowSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | The window for the global write limit, in seconds. |
| `endpointReadLimit` | `number \| null` | No | Read requests allowed for one API key on one endpoint. Set 0 for no limit. |
| `endpointReadWindowSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | The window for the endpoint read limit, in seconds. |
| `endpointWriteLimit` | `number \| null` | No | Write requests allowed for one API key on one endpoint. Set 0 for no limit. |
| `endpointWriteWindowSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | The window for the endpoint write limit, in seconds. |
| `envManagedFields` | `string[] \| null` | No | Fields controlled by environment variables, including _FILE variables. These fields return their effective values. A PUT can repeat these values but cannot change them. |

### saml.get

`settings.saml.get`

Retrieve the SAML SSO configuration. Retrieve the current SAML SSO configuration, including every field exposed in the UI plus the service provider entity ID and ACS return URL. Signing private keys, signing certificates, and identity provider metadata are redacted on read. Requires the SAML feature to be licensed. Needs a licence with feat:saml (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.saml.get({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `entityID` | `string \| null` | No | Service provider entity ID (metadata URL). |
| `returnUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Assertion Consumer Service (ACS) return URL. |
| `mapping` | `object \| null` | No | Mapping of SAML attributes to n8n user fields. |
| `metadata` | `string \| null` | No | Identity provider metadata in XML format. Redacted on read when set because it contains IdP certificates; never echoed back in plaintext. Use an empty string when unset. |
| `metadataUrl` | `string \| null` | No | URL to fetch identity provider metadata from. Use an empty string when unset. |
| `ignoreSSL` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to ignore SSL certificate errors when fetching metadata from a URL. |
| `loginBinding` | `string \| null` | No | SAML login request binding. |
| `loginEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether SAML login is enabled. |
| `loginLabel` | `string \| null` | No | Label shown on the SAML login button. |
| `authnRequestsSigned` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether authentication requests are signed. |
| `wantAssertionsSigned` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether signed assertions are required. |
| `wantMessageSigned` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether signed SAML messages are required. |
| `emailVerifiedRequired` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the identity provider must assert that the user's email address is verified before a login is linked to an existing user by email. When disabled, only an explicit negative assertion is rejected. |
| `signingPrivateKey` | `string \| null` | No | PEM-encoded private key for signing SAML AuthnRequests. Redacted on read when set; never echoed back in plaintext. Use an empty string when unset. |
| `signingCertificate` | `string \| null` | No | PEM-encoded certificate containing the public key matching the signing private key. Redacted on read when set; never echoed back in plaintext. Use an empty string when unset. |
| `acsBinding` | `string \| null` | No | Assertion Consumer Service binding. |
| `signatureConfig` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for the signature in SAML requests and responses. |
| `relayState` | `string \| null` | No | Default relay state value for SAML requests. Use an empty string when unset. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="mapping full type">

```ts
{
  email?: string | null,
  firstName?: string | null,
  lastName?: string | null,
  userPrincipalName?: string | null,
  emailVerified?: string | null,
  n8nInstanceRole?: string | null,
  n8nProjectRoles?: string[] | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="signatureConfig full type">

```ts
{
  prefix?: string | null,
  location?: {
    reference?: string | null,
    action?: string | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### saml.update

`settings.saml.update`

Set the SAML SSO configuration. Replace the SAML SSO configuration with the provided full object. Every writable field must be sent; use empty strings or empty arrays when a value is unset. Read-only entityID / returnUrl from GET are ignored if included, so a GET response can be sent back as a PUT body. Needs a licence with feat:saml (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.saml.update({
	mapping: {
		email: 'ada@example.com',
		firstName: 'example',
		lastName: 'example',
		userPrincipalName: 'example',
		emailVerified: 'ada@example.com',
		n8nInstanceRole: 'example',
		n8nProjectRoles: ['example'],
	},
	metadata: 'example',
	metadataUrl: 'https://example.com/callback',
	ignoreSSL: true,
	loginBinding: 'redirect',
	loginEnabled: true,
	loginLabel: 'example',
	authnRequestsSigned: true,
	wantAssertionsSigned: true,
	wantMessageSigned: true,
	emailVerifiedRequired: true,
	signingPrivateKey: 'example',
	signingCertificate: 'example',
	acsBinding: 'redirect',
	signatureConfig: {
		prefix: 'example',
		location: { reference: 'example', action: 'before' },
	},
	relayState: 'example',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `mapping` | `object` | Yes | Mapping of SAML attributes to n8n user fields. Use empty strings / empty arrays for unused attributes. |
| `metadata` | `string` | Yes | Identity provider metadata in XML format. Use an empty string to clear stored metadata (also clears metadataUrl when no URL is provided). Use the redaction placeholder from a prior GET to leave an existing value unchanged. |
| `metadataUrl` | `string` | Yes | URL to fetch identity provider metadata from. Use an empty string to clear a stored URL. |
| `ignoreSSL` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether to ignore SSL certificate errors when fetching metadata from a URL. |
| `loginBinding` | `redirect \| post` | Yes | SAML login request binding. |
| `loginEnabled` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether SAML login is enabled. |
| `loginLabel` | `string` | Yes | Label shown on the SAML login button. |
| `authnRequestsSigned` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether authentication requests are signed. |
| `wantAssertionsSigned` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether signed assertions are required. |
| `wantMessageSigned` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether signed SAML messages are required. |
| `emailVerifiedRequired` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether the identity provider must assert that the user's email address is verified before a login is linked to an existing user by email. When disabled, only an explicit negative assertion is rejected. |
| `signingPrivateKey` | `string` | Yes | PEM-encoded private key for signing SAML AuthnRequests. Use an empty string to clear an existing key, or the redaction placeholder from a prior GET to leave it unchanged. |
| `signingCertificate` | `string` | Yes | PEM-encoded certificate containing the public key matching the signing private key. Use an empty string when unused or to clear an existing certificate. |
| `acsBinding` | `redirect \| post` | Yes | Assertion Consumer Service binding. |
| `signatureConfig` | `object` | Yes | Configuration for the signature in SAML requests and responses. |
| `relayState` | `string` | Yes | Default relay state value for SAML requests. Use an empty string when unused. |
| `entityID` | `string` | No | Service provider entity ID. Returned by GET for convenience; ignored on write so a GET response can be sent back as a PUT body. |
| `returnUrl` | `string` | No | Assertion Consumer Service return URL. Returned by GET for convenience; ignored on write so a GET response can be sent back as a PUT body. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="mapping full type">

```ts
{
  email: string,
  firstName: string,
  lastName: string,
  userPrincipalName: string,
  emailVerified: string,
  n8nInstanceRole: string,
  n8nProjectRoles: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="signatureConfig full type">

```ts
{
  prefix: string,
  location: {
    reference: string,
    action: before | after | prepend | append
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `entityID` | `string \| null` | No | Service provider entity ID (metadata URL). |
| `returnUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Assertion Consumer Service (ACS) return URL. |
| `mapping` | `object \| null` | No | Mapping of SAML attributes to n8n user fields. |
| `metadata` | `string \| null` | No | Identity provider metadata in XML format. Redacted on read when set because it contains IdP certificates; never echoed back in plaintext. Use an empty string when unset. |
| `metadataUrl` | `string \| null` | No | URL to fetch identity provider metadata from. Use an empty string when unset. |
| `ignoreSSL` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to ignore SSL certificate errors when fetching metadata from a URL. |
| `loginBinding` | `string \| null` | No | SAML login request binding. |
| `loginEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether SAML login is enabled. |
| `loginLabel` | `string \| null` | No | Label shown on the SAML login button. |
| `authnRequestsSigned` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether authentication requests are signed. |
| `wantAssertionsSigned` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether signed assertions are required. |
| `wantMessageSigned` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether signed SAML messages are required. |
| `emailVerifiedRequired` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the identity provider must assert that the user's email address is verified before a login is linked to an existing user by email. When disabled, only an explicit negative assertion is rejected. |
| `signingPrivateKey` | `string \| null` | No | PEM-encoded private key for signing SAML AuthnRequests. Redacted on read when set; never echoed back in plaintext. Use an empty string when unset. |
| `signingCertificate` | `string \| null` | No | PEM-encoded certificate containing the public key matching the signing private key. Redacted on read when set; never echoed back in plaintext. Use an empty string when unset. |
| `acsBinding` | `string \| null` | No | Assertion Consumer Service binding. |
| `signatureConfig` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for the signature in SAML requests and responses. |
| `relayState` | `string \| null` | No | Default relay state value for SAML requests. Use an empty string when unset. |

### securityPolicy.get

`settings.securityPolicy.get`

Retrieve the security policy. Retrieve the instance security policy: personal-space publishing and sharing, the execution-data redaction enforcement floor, and the read-only usage counts shown in the UI. Requires the Personal Space Policy feature to be licensed. Requires the securitySettings:manage API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:personalSpacePolicy (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.securityPolicy.get({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `personalSpacePublishing` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether members may publish workflows and agents from their personal space. |
| `personalSpaceSharing` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether members may share workflows and credentials from their personal space. |
| `publishedPersonalWorkflowsCount` | `number \| null` | No | Number of currently published personal workflows, shown for awareness before tightening the policy. |
| `sharedPersonalWorkflowsCount` | `number \| null` | No | Number of personal workflows currently shared with other users. |
| `sharedPersonalCredentialsCount` | `number \| null` | No | Number of personal credentials currently shared with other users. |
| `redactionEnforcement` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="redactionEnforcement full type">

```ts
{
  floor?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### securityPolicy.update

`settings.securityPolicy.update`

Set the security policy. Replace the instance security policy with the provided full object. Every writable field must be sent. Read-only usage counts from GET are ignored if included, so a GET response can be sent back as a PUT body. The update takes effect exactly as it would from the UI, using the same validation. Needs a licence with feat:personalSpacePolicy (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.settings.securityPolicy.update({
	personalSpacePublishing: true,
	personalSpaceSharing: true,
	redactionEnforcement: { floor: 'off' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `personalSpacePublishing` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether members may publish workflows and agents from their personal space. |
| `personalSpaceSharing` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether members may share workflows and credentials from their personal space. |
| `redactionEnforcement` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `publishedPersonalWorkflowsCount` | `number` | No | Read-only usage count returned by GET. Ignored on write so a GET response can be sent back as a PUT body. |
| `sharedPersonalWorkflowsCount` | `number` | No | Read-only usage count returned by GET. Ignored on write so a GET response can be sent back as a PUT body. |
| `sharedPersonalCredentialsCount` | `number` | No | Read-only usage count returned by GET. Ignored on write so a GET response can be sent back as a PUT body. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="redactionEnforcement full type">

```ts
{
  floor: off | production | all
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `personalSpacePublishing` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether members may publish workflows and agents from their personal space. |
| `personalSpaceSharing` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether members may share workflows and credentials from their personal space. |
| `publishedPersonalWorkflowsCount` | `number \| null` | No | Number of currently published personal workflows, shown for awareness before tightening the policy. |
| `sharedPersonalWorkflowsCount` | `number \| null` | No | Number of personal workflows currently shared with other users. |
| `sharedPersonalCredentialsCount` | `number \| null` | No | Number of personal credentials currently shared with other users. |
| `redactionEnforcement` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="redactionEnforcement full type">

```ts
{
  floor?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Source Control

### getStatus

`sourceControl.getStatus`

Preview pending source control changes. Previews the pending changes between the instance and the connected Git branch in either the push or pull direction. Requires the sourceControl:read API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:sourceControl (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.sourceControl.getStatus({ direction: 'push' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `direction` | `push \| pull` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  file?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  type?: string | null,
  status?: string | null,
  location?: string | null,
  conflict?: boolean | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null,
  pushed?: boolean | null,
  isLocalPublished?: boolean | null,
  isRemoteArchived?: boolean | null,
  parentFolderId?: string | null | null,
  folderPath?: string[] | null,
  remoteFolderPath?: string[] | null,
  owner?: {
    type?: string | null,
    projectId?: string | null,
    projectName?: string | null
  } | null,
  publishingError?: string | null,
  publishingErrorDetails?: {
    reason?: string | null,
    workflowReviewRequestId?: string | null,
    versionId?: string | null
  } | null,
  contentImportPolicy?: {
    violations?: {
      kind?: string | null,
      checkId?: string | null,
      message?: string | null,
      subject?: string | null,
      subjectType?: string | null,
      scope?: string | null,
      matchedRuleId?: string | null,
      projectId?: string | null,
      optInAvailable?: boolean | null,
      limit?: number | null,
      usage?: number | null,
      resetsAt?: string | null
    }[] | null,
    checkErrors?: {
      checkId?: string | null,
      correlationId?: string | null
    }[] | null

}[] | null
```

### pull

`sourceControl.pull`

Pull changes from the remote repository. Fetches changes from the connected Git branch into the instance. Requires the sourceControl:pull API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:sourceControl (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.sourceControl.pull({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `force` | `boolean` | No | Discard local changes and force the pull to complete. |
| `autoPublish` | `none \| all \| published` | No | Controls automatic workflow publishing after import: - `none`: Keep workflows in their local published state (default) - `all`: Publish all imported workflows - `published`: Publish only workflows that were published locally before import |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  file?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  type?: string | null,
  status?: string | null,
  location?: string | null,
  conflict?: boolean | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null,
  pushed?: boolean | null,
  isLocalPublished?: boolean | null,
  isRemoteArchived?: boolean | null,
  parentFolderId?: string | null | null,
  folderPath?: string[] | null,
  remoteFolderPath?: string[] | null,
  owner?: {
    type?: string | null,
    projectId?: string | null,
    projectName?: string | null
  } | null,
  publishingError?: string | null,
  publishingErrorDetails?: {
    reason?: string | null,
    workflowReviewRequestId?: string | null,
    versionId?: string | null
  } | null,
  contentImportPolicy?: {
    violations?: {
      kind?: string | null,
      checkId?: string | null,
      message?: string | null,
      subject?: string | null,
      subjectType?: string | null,
      scope?: string | null,
      matchedRuleId?: string | null,
      projectId?: string | null,
      optInAvailable?: boolean | null,
      limit?: number | null,
      usage?: number | null,
      resetsAt?: string | null
    }[] | null,
    checkErrors?: {
      checkId?: string | null,
      correlationId?: string | null
    }[] | null
  } | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### push

`sourceControl.push`

Push local source control changes. Commits and pushes the selected files to the connected Git branch. Each entry in fileNames is resolved against a fresh preview of the pending changes. Requires the sourceControl:push API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:sourceControl (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.sourceControl.push({
	commitMessage: 'example',
	fileNames: [{ id: 'doc_8f2k1x9q', type: 'credential' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `commitMessage` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `fileNames` | `object[]` | Yes | — |
| `force` | `boolean` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>

```ts
{
  id: string,
  type: credential | workflow | tags | variables | file | folders | project | datatable
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

## Tags

### create

`tags.create`

Create a tag. Create a tag in your instance. Requires the tag:create API key scope.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.tags.create({ name: 'reports' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### delete

## Users

### changeRole

`users.changeRole`

Change a user's global role. Change a user's global role Requires the user:changeRole API key scope. Needs a licence with feat:advancedPermissions (Business or Enterprise plans): without it n8n answers 403.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.users.changeRole({ userId: 'example', newRoleName: 'global:admin' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `userId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the user. |
| `newRoleName` | `global:admin \| global:member \| global:chatUser \| string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | n8n accepted the call; it answered with no body. |



---

### delete

`users.delete`

Delete user. Delete a user from your instance. Requires the user:delete API key scope. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.users.delete({ userId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `userId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the user. |
| `transferId` | `string` | No | ID of the project to transfer the deleted user's workflows and credentials to. Omit to delete those resources instead of transferring them. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | n8n accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

### get

`users.get`

Get user by ID/Email. Retrieve a user from your instance. Only available for the instance owner. Requires the user:read API key scope.


| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

| `role` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### invite

`users.invite`

Create multiple users. Create one or more users. Requires the user:create API key scope. Sends an invitation email to each address when the instance has email set up.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.users.invite({
	users: [{ email: 'ada@example.com' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `users` | `object[]` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="users full type">

```ts
{
  email: string,
  role?: global:admin | global:member | global:chatUser | string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts

  user?: {
    id?: string | null,
    email?: string | null,
    inviteAcceptUrl?: string | null,
    emailSent?: boolean | null,
    role?: string | null
  } | null,
  error?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Workflows

### activate

`workflows.activate`

Publish a workflow. Deprecated: use POST /workflows/&#123;id&#125;/publish instead. Publish a workflow. In n8n v1, this action was termed activating a workflow. Requires the workflow:activate API key scope. Turns the workflow on: its triggers start running it.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.workflows.activate({ workflowId: '2tUt1wbLX592XDdX' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `workflowId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the workflow |
| `versionId` | `string` | No | The specific version ID to activate or publish. If not provided, the latest version is used. |
| `name` | `string` | No | Optional name for the workflow version during activation. |
| `description` | `string` | No | Optional description for the workflow version during activation. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `description` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `active` | `boolean \| null` | No | Deprecated. Check whether `activeVersionId` is not null instead. |
| `activeVersionId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `isArchived` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `versionId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `versionCounter` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `sourceWorkflowId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `triggerCount` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `nodes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Nodes that make up the workflow |
| `connections` | `object \| null` | No | Connections between nodes, keyed by source node name |
| `nodeGroups` | `object[] \| null` | No | Visual groupings of nodes shown as frames on the canvas |
| `settings` | `object \| null` | No | Execution and behaviour settings for the workflow |
| `staticData` | `object \| null` | No | Data the workflow keeps between executions |
| `pinData` | `object \| null` | No | Pinned sample data for nodes, keyed by node name |
| `meta` | `object \| null` | No | Workflow metadata such as template information |
| `tags` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `activeVersion` | `object \| null` | No | — |

### create

`workflows.create`

Create a workflow. Create a workflow in your instance. Requires the workflow:create API key scope.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.workflows.create({
	name: 'Hello webhook',
	nodes: [{
		id: '1f2e3d4c-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',
		name: 'Webhook',
		type: 'n8n-nodes-base.webhook',
		typeVersion: 2,
		position: [0, 0],
		parameters: { path: 'hello', httpMethod: 'GET' },
	}],
	connections: {},
	settings: { executionOrder: 'v1' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `nodes` | `object[]` | Yes | Nodes that make up the workflow |
| `connections` | `object` | Yes | Connections between nodes, keyed by source node name |
| `settings` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `nodeGroups` | `object[]` | No | Visual groupings of nodes shown as frames on the canvas |
| `staticData` | `string \| Record<string, any> \| any` | No | Data the workflow keeps between executions |
| `pinData` | `Record<string, any> \| null` | No | Pinned sample data for nodes, keyed by node name |
| `projectId` | `string` | No | Target project to create the workflow in. Defaults to the user's personal project. |
| `parentFolderId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the folder to place the workflow in. Omit or null to place at the project root. |
| `shared` | `object[]` | No | Accepted for compatibility and ignored. The owner share is derived from the target project. |
| `description` | `string` | No | Description of the workflow |

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

| `nextCursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

### publish

`workflows.publish`

Publish a workflow. Publish a workflow. In n8n v1, this action was termed activating a workflow. Requires the workflow:activate API key scope. Publishes the current version: its triggers start running it.

**Risk:** `destructive`

| `description` | `string` | No | Optional description for the workflow version during publication |

### run

`workflows.run`

Run a workflow with existing run data. Start a manual execution of the latest saved version of a workflow. n8n reuses the given run data and runs the nodes up to the destination node. The call returns when the execution starts. It does not wait for the execution to finish. The user must also have the workflow:execute project scope. Runs the workflow now; its nodes may call outside services or spend credits there. Added in n8n 2.50; older instances answer 404.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.workflows.run({
	workflowId: '2tUt1wbLX592XDdX',
	runData: {},
	destinationNode: { nodeName: 'example', mode: 'inclusive' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Safe retry key for this POST. Use 1 to 128 visible ASCII characters. The same key and the same request return the first successful response. A different request with this key returns 422. A retry while the first request is in progress returns 409. |
| `workflowId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the workflow |
| `runData` | `object` | Yes | Run data to reuse, keyed by node name. Use `data.resultData.runData` from `GET /executions/{id}?includeData=true`. n8n reuses the output of these nodes and runs the other nodes up to the destination node. If the run data does not reach the destination node from a trigger, n8n runs the workflow from its trigger. If that trigger needs an external event to start, the API returns `409`. |
| `destinationNode` | `object` | Yes | The node where the run stops. |
| `dirtyNodeNames` | `string[]` | No | Names of nodes that changed since the run data was made. n8n runs these nodes again and does not reuse their run data. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="runData full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="destinationNode full type">

```ts
{
  nodeName: string,
  mode: inclusive | exclusive
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `executionId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the new execution. Use `GET /executions/{id}` to read its status. |

### transfer

`workflows.transfer`

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts

```

| `destinationProjectId` | `string` | Yes | — |

### update

`workflows.update`

Update a workflow. Update a workflow. If the workflow is published, the updated version will be automatically re-published unless publishIfActive is set to false.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.n8n.api.workflows.update({
	workflowId: '2tUt1wbLX592XDdX',
	name: 'example',
	nodes: [{}],
	connections: {},
	settings: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `workflowId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the workflow |
| `publishIfActive` | `boolean` | No | Whether to publish the update if the workflow is currently published. Set to `false` to save the change as a draft on the existing published version instead of releasing it. Has no effect on a workflow that isn't currently published. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `nodes` | `object[]` | Yes | Nodes that make up the workflow |
| `connections` | `object` | Yes | Connections between nodes, keyed by source node name |
| `settings` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `nodeGroups` | `object[]` | No | Visual groupings of nodes shown as frames on the canvas |
| `staticData` | `string \| Record<string, any> \| any` | No | Data the workflow keeps between executions |
| `pinData` | `Record<string, any> \| null` | No | Pinned sample data for nodes, keyed by node name |
| `parentFolderId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the folder to move the workflow into. Pass null to move it to the project root; omit to leave its current folder unchanged. |
| `shared` | `object[]` | No | Accepted for compatibility and ignored. The owner share is derived from the target project. |
| `description` | `string` | No | Description of the workflow |
